*Yuming Feng,Frederick Pu,One An,Osbert Bastani,Li Zhang,Jiani Huang,Xujie Si,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LCS-Bench，一个基于《计算机科学逻辑》教材的理论级自动形式化基准，覆盖327个教材条目、4076条Lean声明和超过85K行Lean代码。通过半自动化智能体流水线构建，包含14个模型的评估表明该基准高质量、一致且忠实，但现有最先进模型仅达到20.1%的自动形式化任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化工作主要聚焦于孤立语句，而理论级自动形式化（连贯翻译数百个相互依赖的定义、引理和定理）由于在一致性、忠实性、可扩展性和正确性方面存在挑战，尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，构建一个专门的、理论级的基准测试。

Method: 通过半自动化智能体流水线构建LCS-Bench，具体包括：概念图利用、形式化签名规划、问题追踪、反例搜索辅助的sorry填充，以及人类专家的忠实性审查。同时设计了数据引擎自动衍生出五个评估轨道的基准测试，并引入基于定义等价性检查器的新型评估协议。

Result: LCS-Bench包含327个教材条目、4076条Lean声明、超过85K行Lean代码。在14个模型上的评估表明：该基准高质量、一致且忠实；具有挑战性，最先进模型仅达到20.1%的自动形式化任务准确率。

Conclusion: LCS-Bench为理论级自动形式化提供了具有挑战性的标准化评估平台；分析揭示了理论级自动形式化的关键发现，并为未来研究指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Auto-formalization is critical for scalable formal verification, but existing progress largely focuses on isolated statements, while theory-scale auto-formalization, which coherently translates hundreds of interdependent definitions, lemmas, and theorems, remains open due to challenges in consistency, faithfulness, scalability, and correctness. In this paper, we introduce LCS-Bench, a stand-alone, theory-scale benchmark based on Logics for Computer Science. LCS-Bench is built through a novel semi-automated agentic pipeline that leverages concept graphs, formal signature planning, issue tracking, sorry-filling with counter-example search, complemented by faithfulness review from human experts. The resulting artifact covers 327 textbook items, over 4,076 Lean declarations, and more than 85K lines of Lean code. The dataset supports broad evaluation through a data engine that automatically derives five tracks of evaluation benchmarks, measuring different aspects of auto-formalization and theorem-proving capabilities. We also introduce a novel evaluation protocol featuring definitional equivalence checkers, enabling more fine-grained and faithful assessment. Through extensive evaluation on 14 models, we demonstrate that (1) LCS-Bench is of high quality, consistent, and faithful; (2) the benchmark is challenging, with state-of-the-art models achieving only 20.1% on auto-formalization tasks; and (3) our analysis reveals key findings regarding theory-scale auto-formalization and suggests promising directions for future work.

</details>

*Wenjie Huang,Yang Li,Jingjia Teng,Mingwei Jin,Kai Song,Yougang Bian,Yongfu Li,Qisong Yang,Helai Huang*

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向自动驾驶高速公路变道的安全迁移强化学习框架，通过自适应教师干预机制、教师引导的安全迁移模块和加权优化机制，在多种交通密度和真实数据集下实现了安全性和学习效率的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移学习方法在自动驾驶变道决策中面临两个主要局限：1) 源域与目标域之间的分布偏移导致迁移失配，引发训练震荡和性能下降；2) 目标域适配依赖探索性交互，在安全关键的变道场景中难以保证训练安全性。

Method: 1) 基于瞬时安全成本的自适应教师干预机制，约束风险探索并逐步减弱干预强度，同时生成双源样本用于联合训练；2) 教师引导的安全迁移模块，通过奖励塑形将教师策略的动作评估信息融入学生策略学习；3) 教师引导的加权优化机制，利用似然比因子调整策略优化中的样本权重以稳定迁移性能。

Result: 在多种交通密度和真实NGSIM数据集上的实验表明，该方法在安全性上超越基线方法52.2%以上，学习效率提升5.0%以上。

Conclusion: 验证了所提出的安全感知迁移策略在自动驾驶高速公路变道任务中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transfer learning improves policy learning efficiency by reusing knowledge from source tasks, providing a feasible paradigm for safe and efficient autonomous highway lane changing decision-making. Existing methods frequently encounter transfer mismatch induced by distribution shifts between source and target domains, leading to training oscillation and performance decline. Besides, target domain adaptation depends on exploratory interactions, which struggles to guarantee training safety in safety-critical lane changing cases. To tackle these limitations, this paper proposes a safe transfer reinforcement learning framework for autonomous highway lane changing. First, we design an adaptive teacher intervention mechanism based on instantaneous safety cost to restrain risky exploration and fade intervention strength progressively, with theoretical analysis on return bounds for mixed behavior policy. This intervention also produces dual-source samples for joint training. Second, a teacher-guided safe transfer module embeds action evaluation information of teacher policy into student learning via reward shaping to boost training safety and efficiency, with teacher guidance decaying as policy safety rises. Third, a teacher-guided weighted optimization mechanism adjusts sample weights in policy optimization using a likelihood ratio factor to stabilize transfer performance. Experiments under varied traffic densities and validations on real-world NGSIM dataset reveal that our method surpasses baseline approaches by over 52.2% in safety and 5.0% in learning efficiency. Results verify the efficacy and robustness of our safety-aware transfer strategy for autonomous highway lane changing under various traffic conditions.

</details>


### [42] [CascadeFormer: Depth-Tapered Transformers Motivated by Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26538)
*Huzama Ahmad,Cao Viet Hai Nam,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种利用深度Transformer中深层网络贡献度低这一不对称性的效率方法：CascadeFormer（逐层递减宽度）和CascadeFlow Pruning（基于累积训练梯度剪枝），并提出Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry (GFA)理论解释深层贡献不足的原因——Pre-LayerNorm残差结构中梯度扇入随深度线性衰减（深度监督下为二次衰减），通过从零训练至1.2B参数的实验提供了相关性和干预性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度Transformer中均匀堆叠的残差块在深层往往贡献有限，造成计算浪费。作者旨在利用这种不对称性设计更高效的架构，并揭示其背后的理论机制。

Method: 1. CascadeFormer：随深度递减网络宽度，匹配不均匀的信息流动；2. CascadeFlow Pruning：利用累积训练梯度进行层剪枝，无需事后分析；3. 提出GFA理论：在Pre-LayerNorm残差堆叠中，梯度 = 恒等路径 + 所有下游功能路径之和，导致梯度扇入随深度线性衰减（深度监督下二次衰减），早期层梯度更丰富。

Result: CascadeFormer在相同训练预算下困惑度与基线相当，延迟降低8.6%，吞吐量提升9.4%；CascadeFlow Pruning在困惑度和秩稳定性上优于标准启发式方法，在下游准确率上保持竞争力。GFA理论通过从零训练至1.2B参数的实验获得相关性和干预性证据。

Conclusion: 更深层梯度扇入的衰减是结构性的而非幅度性的：等化逐层梯度范数无法恢复深层价值，而通过参数共享重复增加下游路径数则可恢复并提升深层贡献。梯度幅度能否在高层秩区域之外代理扇入，以及百亿参数规模下的动态行为，仍有待探索。

Abstract: Deep Transformers are composed of uniformly stacked residual blocks, yet their deepest layers often add little value. We present two efficiency methods that exploit this asymmetry. CascadeFormer tapers width with depth to match the uneven information flow across layers, achieving comparable perplexity to a uniform baseline at the same training budget while reducing latency by 8.6% and increasing throughput by 9.4%. CascadeFlow Pruning removes layers using accumulated training gradients, with no post hoc analysis. It outperforms standard heuristics on perplexity and rank-stability and stays competitive on downstream accuracy. To motivate these methods, we propose Gradient Fan-in Asymmetry (GFA) as a structural account of why deeper layers contribute less. In Pre-LayerNorm residual stacks, the gradient at a layer is the sum of an identity path and all downstream functional paths, producing a gradient fan-in that decays linearly with depth (and quadratically under deep supervision), yielding richer gradients for early layers and sparser ones for later layers. We provide correlational and interventional evidence for GFA on models trained from scratch up to 1.2B parameters. Across Transformers and ResNets, accumulated training gradients follow the theoretical fan-in and are associated with post hoc layer importance. Two interventions point to structure rather than magnitude as the bottleneck: equalizing per-layer gradient norms does not restore late-layer value, while increasing downstream path counts via parameter-shared repetition restores and elevates it. Whether gradient magnitude proxies fan-in beyond high-rank regimes, and how these dynamics behave at the 100B+ scale, remain open questions.

</details>


### [43] [Can Large Language Models Reliably Code Qualitative Humanitarian Data? A Benchmark Study Against Human Expert Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26541)
*Jerome Marston,Tino Kreutzer,Salomé Garnier,Ella Boone,Phuong N Pham,Patrick Vinck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过基准测试评估了46个大语言模型在编码人道主义定性数据方面的可靠性，发现多个LLM在结构化提示和推理配置下能达到与人类编码者相当的信度水平，但聚合信度指标不足以指导部署决策，模型在间接表达需求、类别外需求及保护相关问题上存在差异。研究建议LLM应作为人类判断的补充而非替代，并需要结构化编码手册、推理模型及分级监督机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义组织在规模化分析受影响人群的定性数据时缺乏足够的人员、时间和专业知识，而LLM可能扩展这一能力，但其可靠性尚未被直接验证。

Method: 使用150个高保真合成人道主义对话记录，将46个LLM与人类黄金标准进行比较，评估结合了Krippendorff's alpha信度检验、正确/接近正确/错误编码的差异分析，以及针对人道主义特定标准（如歧视、复杂需求层级、非标准沟通风格）的定性评估。

Result: 多个LLM在执行演绎编码任务时能达到与经验丰富的人类编码者相当的信度水平，尤其是在使用结构化提示和启用推理配置的情况下；但模型在间接表达需求、预设类别外需求以及安全与歧视等保护相关问题上表现不一。

Conclusion: LLM能够实质性扩大人道主义分析能力，但不能替代人类判断。恰当使用需要结构化编码手册、推理模型、关注特定主题表现，以及对编码错误后果最严重的类别实施分级监督。对于敏感数据，开源权重模型部署在自托管基础设施上是结合分析可扩展性与数据治理的可行路径。

Abstract: Data from affected populations are crucial for informing humanitarian response, but their value depends on timely and consistent interpretation of nuanced accounts of need. Humanitarian organizations often lack the staff, time, and specialist expertise required to analyze this information at scale. Large language models (LLMs) may expand this capacity, but their reliability for coding qualitative humanitarian data has not been directly established. This benchmark study compares 46 LLMs to a human Gold Standard using 150 high-fidelity synthetic humanitarian transcripts. Evaluation combined inter-rater reliability testing with Krippendorff's alpha, discrepancy analysis distinguishing correct, near-correct, and incorrect codes, and qualitative assessment across humanitarian-specific criteria including discrimination, complex needs hierarchies, and non-standard communication styles. The authors find that multiple LLMs can perform deductive coding at reliability levels comparable to experienced human coders, especially when structured prompts and reasoning-enabled configurations are used. At the same time, aggregate reliability metrics alone are insufficient for deployment decisions. Models varied in recognizing needs expressed indirectly, needs outside predefined categories, and protection-relevant concerns such as physical safety and discrimination. These findings suggest that LLMs can materially expand humanitarian analytical capacity, but not as substitutes for human judgment. Appropriate use requires structured codebooks, reasoning-enabled models, attention to theme-specific performance, and tiered oversight focused on categories where miscoding would have the greatest programmatic consequences. For sensitive humanitarian data, open-weights models deployed on self-hosted infrastructure may offer a viable path for combining analytical scalability with stronger data governance.

</details>


### [44] [Revisiting Action Factorization for Complex Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26574)
*Timothy Flavin,Sandip Sen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对混合离散-连续动作空间中的因子化方法进行了横向比较研究，在三种算法（PPO、SAC、DQN）和三种动作空间（离散化、混合、连续）上评估了六种因子化方法，涵盖了220种配置，并提出了两种新环境以及VDN-PPO和PPO-MIX变体。结果表明分支决斗架构在计算与性能间取得最佳平衡，自回归动作和原生连续SAC分别达到最高性能，但计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实控制问题常涉及混合离散-连续动作空间（如自动驾驶、机器人），但现有强化学习基准环境多为统一动作空间配置，而混合动作基准环境大多重量级或仅测试少数方法，缺乏系统性横向比较研究。

Method: 在PPO、SAC、DQN三种算法上，对六种因子化方法（独立网络、共享编码器、VDN、QPLEX、联合、自回归）进行交叉研究，在四个轻量环境（Platform、hybrid-LunarLander、Hybrid-Shoot、CoopPush）中测试，排除无效组合后共220种配置。同时提出两种新C++并行环境，并引入VDN-PPO和PPO-MIX变体（使用分支评价器为多头PPO分配信用）。

Result: 分支决斗架构在计算与性能间最有效；自回归动作方法达到最高性能；原生连续SAC优于离散和混合算法；VDN-PPO和PPO-MIX优于所有其他PPO因子化方法；但自回归和连续SAC计算成本更高。

Conclusion: 混合动作空间的因子化选择需权衡计算成本与性能，分支决斗架构是性价比最优方案，而追求极致性能时自回归或连续SAC是更好选择。

Abstract: Many real-world control problems involve hybrid discrete-continuous action spaces. For example, steering and signaling in autonomous driving, and aiming and firing in robotics or video-games. Despite real-world hybrid factorization and reinforcement learning framework support for complex action spaces (e.g., Gymnasium, PettingZoo, TorchRL, SeedRL, Mujoco, etc), the default environments within those frameworks often implement uniform action space configurations (LunarLander, Walker2D, Cheetah, SMAC, SUMO, Ant, Atari). Landmark hybrid-action benchmarks (RoboCup 2D HFO, SC2LE, Platform, CARLA, etc) are mostly heavyweight or archival implementations originating from papers which test one or a small number of competing factorization methods on one kind of control. This article provides a cross-sectional study of factorization methods [independent networks, shared encoder, VDN, QPLEX, Joint, Auto-Regressive] on each of three families of algorithms [PPO, SAC, DQN] across three action spaces [discretized, hybrid, continuous] over four lightweight environments [Platform, hybrid-LunarLander, Hybrid-Shoot, CoopPush]. Accounting for some invalid pairings such as joint-continuous, we are left with 220 configurations to analyze each method. We provide two new C++ parallel gymnasium and petting-zoo compliant environments [CoopPush, Hybrid-Shoot] to isolate particular challenges such as state-dependent inter-action dependence. Finally, we introduce VDN-PPO and PPO-MIX which use a branching critic to assign credit to multi-headed PPO. These variants out-perform all other tested PPO factorizations. Our results suggest that branching dueling architectures balance compute and performance most effectively, with Auto-Regressive actions reaching the highest performance overall and native continuous SAC outperforming discrete and hybrid algorithms, albiet both at increased computational cost.

</details>


### [45] [SharQ: Bridging Activation Sparsity and FP4 Quantization for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26587)
*Haoqian Meng,Yilun Luo,Yafei Zhao,Wenyuan Liu,Huaqing Zheng,Xindian Ma,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SharQ 是一种无需训练的推理方法，通过在线稀疏-稠密分解将激活稀疏性与 FP4 量化结合，有效恢复了大语言模型在低比特格式下的精度损失，并显著提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代加速器支持低比特浮点格式和半结构化稀疏性，但将两者结合用于 LLM 激活压缩仍面临挑战：FP4 量化中激活异常值主导 block scales，而直接应用 N:M 稀疏掩码会丢弃中等值，耦合了稀疏化损失与量化误差。

Method: SharQ 提出在线稀疏-稠密分解：对每个激活张量生成输入自适应的 N:M 掩码提取异常值主导的稀疏骨干并量化为 FP4，再定义相对于量化后稀疏骨干的稠密残差。两条路径共享同一 FP4 权重载荷但具有路径特定的 scale views，并通过融合预备核将掩码生成、残差构建和层归一化合并为一个算子。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen3-30B-A3B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，SharQ 恢复了 NVFP4 到 FP16 精度差距的 43-63%，并泛化至 NVFP4、HiF4 和 MXFP4 格式。在 RTX 5090 上实现 2.2-2.4× 延迟降低和 1.2-1.4× 吞吐提升，视频生成任务中结合 SageAttention 加速达 1.58×。

Conclusion: SharQ 是一种无需校准数据、重训练或模型特定调优的训练无关推理方法，能有效结合激活稀疏性与 FP4 量化，在多种模型和格式上取得精度恢复与计算加速的双重收益。

Abstract: Low-bit floating-point formats and semi-structured sparsity are increasingly supported by modern accelerators, yet combining them for LLM activation compression remains challenging: activations contain input-dependent outliers that dominate block scales in FP4 quantization, and directly applying N:M sparsity masks discards moderate values, coupling sparsification loss with quantization error. We introduce SharQ, a training-free inference method that bridges activation sparsity and FP4 quantization through an online sparse--dense decomposition. For each activation tensor, SharQ generates an input-adaptive N:M mask to extract an outlier-dominated sparse backbone, quantizes it to FP4, and defines a dense residual relative to the quantized sparse backbone rather than the unquantized sparse values. A sparse FP4 GEMM processes the backbone while a dense FP4 GEMM compensates for both mask-induced activation loss and sparse-path quantization error. The two paths share a single FP4 weight payload with path-specific scale views, and a fused preparation kernel absorbs mask generation, residual construction, and layer normalization into one operator. SharQ requires no calibration data, retraining, or model-specific tuning. Evaluated on Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen3-30B-A3B, and Qwen3-VL-8B, SharQ recovers 43--63% of the NVFP4-to-FP16 accuracy gap across language and vision-language tasks, and generalizes across NVFP4, HiF4, and MXFP4 formats. On an RTX 5090, SharQ delivers 2.2--2.4$\times$ latency reduction over FP16 and 1.2--1.4$\times$ throughput improvement over FP8 in language model serving, and up to 1.58$\times$ speedup on Wan2.2-T2V-A14B video generation when combined with SageAttention. Our code is available at https://github.com/actypedef/SharQ.

</details>


### [46] [Empirical Software Engineering TerraProbe: A Layered-Oracle Framework for Detecting Deceptive Fixes in LLM-Assisted Terraform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26590)
*Manar Alsaid,Chimdumebi Nebolisa,Faris Abbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TerraProbe五层评估框架，用于检测LLM辅助的Terraform安全修复是否真正有效。通过对288个修复样本的分析发现，仅依赖静态扫描工具（如Checkov）会高估修复成功率（83.3%），但实际计划有效性仅39.6%，且71.4%的修复属于"欺骗性修复"——通过自动化检查但未真正解决安全漏洞。三个主流模型（gemini-2.5、GPT-4o、Claude 3.5）的欺骗性修复率无统计学差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在评估LLM修复Terraform安全配置错误时，仅以静态分析工具告警消失作为修复成功标准，忽略了修复计划的有效性、行为变更及安全意图，导致对LLM修复能力的误判。

Method: 构建TerraProbe五层评估框架：1）目标Checkov移除；2）全扫描器清洁；3）Terraform计划有效性；4）计划行为对比；5）人工裁决。对68个真实TerraDS模块和28个受控注入缺陷模块，使用三个LLM生成288个修复样本，进行多层评估。

Result: 目标Checkov移除率达83.3%，但全扫描器清洁度仅10.4%，计划成功率39.6%，计划对比可达38.5%。人工裁决显示71.4%的真实修复为欺骗性修复。三个模型的欺骗性修复率（57.1%-71.4%）无显著差异（Fisher p>0.10）。所有CKV2_AWS_11欺骗性修复案例中通配符Resource权限问题均未解决。

Conclusion: 单层静态扫描评估严重高估LLM安全修复效果。TerraProbe框架能有效区分真正意图对齐的安全修复与欺骗性修复。LLM在Terraform安全修复中普遍存在欺骗性修复问题，且当前主流模型表现无差异。

Abstract: Security misconfigurations in Terraform Infrastructure-as-Code are a growing risk in cloud deployments, and large language models are increasingly used as automated repair agents. Existing evaluations often treat a repair as successful when the targeted static-analysis finding disappears, without checking planning validity, behavioral change, or security intent. This paper presents TerraProbe, a five-layer oracle framework for evaluating LLM-assisted Terraform security repair. We apply TerraProbe to 288 first-pass repairs generated by gemini-2.5-flash-lite, GPT-4o, and Claude 3.5 Sonnet across 68 real-world TerraDS modules and 28 controlled injected-defect modules. The results show that targeted Checkov removal overstates repair success. Although targeted removal reaches 83.3 percent for the primary model, full-scanner cleanliness drops to 10.4 percent, Terraform planning succeeds for 39.6 percent, and plan comparison is reachable for 38.5 percent. Human adjudication further shows that 71.4 percent of plan-compared real-world repairs are deceptive fixes that pass automated checks while leaving the underlying vulnerability in place. This pattern is statistically indistinguishable across the three models, with deceptive-fix rates from 57.1 percent to 71.4 percent and pairwise Fisher exact p-values above 0.10. The paper introduces a four-dimensional taxonomy of deceptive fixes, validated with Cohen kappa of 0.78 and Krippendorff alpha of 0.76. IAM permission analysis confirms that wildcard Resource grants persist in all nine CKV2 AWS 11 deceptive-fix cases. TerraProbe contributes an evaluation methodology, a replication package, and the Multi-Layer Oracle Evaluation framework for distinguishing intent-aligned security repairs from scanner-passing false successes.

</details>


### [47] [Sketched Linear Contrastive Learning: Approximation, Optimization, and Statistical Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26617)
*Ziyan Chen,Zhongzhu Zhou,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对比表示学习中的缩放定律，针对双高斯潜变量设置下的草化线性模型，推导了关于草化维度、样本量和优化有效视野的显式缩放律，揭示了对比学习与标准线性回归不同的缩放行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律对于理解模型规模、数据规模和计算量如何影响学习性能至关重要。虽然线性回归的缩放定律已有理论工作，但对比表示学习的缩放行为尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用配对高斯潜变量设置下的草化线性模型；学习者仅观察两个相关变量的草化视图，通过全批次经验梯度下降训练双线性对比评分；分析对齐幂律谱和高斯负二次对比代理下的风险分解（不可约风险、逼近误差、GD偏差、GD方差及交叉项）。

Result: 推导出关于草化维度M、样本量N和有效优化视野L_eff γ的显式缩放律；交叉项由偏差和方差控制，不影响上界缩放；对比学习需学习两个视图间的交互，改变了优化和有限样本噪声随模型规模、数据和训练时间缩放的方式。

Conclusion: 为对比学习中缩放行为的理解提供了首个理论步骤，为平衡模型规模、数据和优化计算给出了指导。

Abstract: Scaling laws describe how learning performance varies with model size, data size, and compute. While recent theoretical work has established scaling laws for sketched linear regression, much less is understood for contrastive representation learning. In this paper, we study a sketched linear model for contrastive learning under a paired Gaussian latent-variable setup. The learner observes only sketched views of two correlated variables and trains a bilinear contrastive score by full-batch empirical gradient descent. We analyze a Gaussian-negative quadratic contrastive surrogate under aligned power-law spectra and a contrastive source condition, where we derive a risk decomposition into irreducible risk, approximation error, GD bias, GD variance, and a cross term. The cross term is controlled by the bias and variance and therefore does not affect the upper-bound scaling. Our main theorem gives an explicit scaling law with respect to sketch dimension $M$, sample size $N$, and effective optimization horizon $L_{\mathrm{eff}}γ$. Compared with standard linear-regression scaling laws, the contrastive setting must learn interactions between two views, and this changes how optimization and finite-sample noise scale with model size, data, and training time. This provides a first theoretical step toward understanding scaling behavior in contrastive learning and gives guidance for balancing model size, data, and optimization compute.

</details>


### [48] [Discovering Millions of Interpretable Features with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26620)
*XinYang He,Wei Wang,Bing Zhao,Xuan Ren,WenBo Li,WeiXu Qiao,Hu Wei,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Qwen3-Instruct SAE，一套针对Qwen3指令微调模型家族（1.7B、4B、8B）训练的稀疏自编码器套件，覆盖残差流、MLP输出和注意力输出等多个激活位点，并通过拒绝行为引导案例展示了其在因果干预中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE在分解语言模型叠加表征方面效果显著，但训练成本高且开源模型有限，因此需要为Qwen3指令微调模型提供一套全面的SAE资源，以支持稀疏表征与特征级机制研究。

Method: 对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B在残差流、MLP输出和注意力输出三个关键激活位点训练逐层SAE；对Qwen3-8B在部分残差流层训练SAE。使用激活级重建指标和模型级恢复指标系统评估SAE，并通过拒绝行为引导案例验证其效用。

Result: 揭示了不同层和组件之间稀疏性与保真度的权衡关系；在拒绝行为引导案例中，选定的SAE特征能够因果性地引导指令微调Qwen3模型产生拒绝行为。

Conclusion: Qwen3-Instruct SAE为研究指令微调语言模型中的稀疏表征、特征级机制和行为干预提供了实用资源。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful tool for decomposing superposed language model representations into sparse and interpretable features. However, training SAEs is computationally expensive, and available open-source SAE models remain limited. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Qwen3-Instruct SAE}, a comprehensive suite of SAEs trained on the Qwen3 instruction-tuned model family, covering Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B. For Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B, we train layer-wise SAEs at three key activation sites: residual streams, MLP outputs, and attention outputs. For Qwen3-8B, we train SAEs on a subset of residual stream layers. We systematically evaluate these SAEs using both activation-level reconstruction metrics and model-level recovery metrics, revealing distinct sparsity--fidelity trade-offs across layers and components. Finally, we demonstrate the utility of Qwen3-Instruct SAE through a refusal-steering case study, showing that selected SAE features can causally steer instruction-tuned Qwen3 models toward refusal behavior. Our release provides a practical resource for studying sparse representations, feature-level mechanisms, and behavioral interventions in instruction-tuned language models

</details>

*Evan Ning,Wei Xue,Dong Lou,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在激活空间中使用预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为单语义特征字典进行正则化，以缓解大语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，相比传统权重空间正则化方法（如EWC）更有效且更节省内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间正则化方法（如EWC）在大语言模型上表现不佳，作者认为部分原因是LLM的"多语义"特性导致逐权重的重要性估计过于粗糙，无法隔离需要保护的知识。

Method: 使用预训练的稀疏自编码器作为单语义特征字典，在模型激活空间进行正则化；从约束优化角度推导新的损失函数，显式平衡稳定性和可塑性；仅使用当前任务数据计算SAE特征掩码并保留，无需先前任务数据。

Result: 在TRACE和MedCL持续学习基准上，该方法在不引入任务特定架构组件的情况下取得了最强结果，超越了EWC等传统权重空间正则化方法；同时实证表明SAE特征空间中任务相关表示是线性可分的，而权重空间中则无法区分。

Conclusion: 激活空间正则化比权重空间正则化更适合大语言模型的持续学习，SAE提供的单语义特征表示能更精确地保护任务相关知识，且方法更具内存效率。

Abstract: Weight-space regularization methods such as Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) are the standard approach to catastrophic forgetting in continual learning. However, those methods tend to underperform when applied to large language models. We argue that such underperformance can be partly explained by the ``polysemantic'' nature of large language models: per-weight importance estimates utilized by EWC-style regularization are too coarse and cannot isolate the knowledge that needs protection. In this paper, we propose regularizing instead in the model's activation space, using pretrained Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a monosemantic feature dictionary. From the perspective of constrained optimization, we derive a new loss function that uses the SAE feature dictionary to explicitly balance stability and plasticity, and show that EWC is a special case in the one-sided weight-space penalty setting. Unlike replay-based methods that store or revisit examples from earlier tasks, our method requires no previous-task data after mask construction: current-task data is used to compute a compact SAE feature mask, and only this mask is retained for later training. Further, since the feature space has significantly lower dimensionality than the parameter space, the proposed method is more memory efficient. On the TRACE and MedCL continual learning benchmarks, the method achieves the strongest result among approaches without introducing task-specific architectural components, also surpassing traditional weight-space regularization methods like EWC. Beyond performance comparisons, we provide empirical evidence for the polysemanticity thesis: task-relevant representations are linearly separable in the SAE feature basis but indistinguishable from chance in the weight basis, and weight-space protection is nearly non-selective at the concept level.

</details>


### [50] [Target-Aware Bandit Allocation for Scalable Surrogate Optimization in Chemical Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26657)
*Mohammad Haddadnia,Yuvan Chali,Abhilash Jayaraj,Constance Kraay,Joana Reis,Felix Strieth-Kalthoff,Haribabu Arthanari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BOBa是一个结合多臂老虎机与代理模型的优化框架，通过自适应分配计算资源到动作空间分区，在不进行全库推理的情况下实现高效的高价值候选筛选。实验表明，基于不确定性乐观的强盗算法结合有意义的分区策略，能够在推理成本与筛选性能之间实现可调节的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构药物发现等科学领域，从海量离散空间（数十亿至万亿级化合物库）中识别高价值候选物是一个核心挑战。尽管代理模型可以降低昂贵评估的次数，但对整个库进行代理推理本身已成为计算瓶颈。现有方法无法避免全库推理，亟需一种能自适应分配计算资源、避免扫描整个库的优化框架。

Method: 提出BOBa（Bandit-guided Surrogate Optimization Framework），核心思想是将动作空间划分为多个分区，并将每个分区视为多臂老虎机（MAB）中的一个臂。通过乐观不确定性（optimism-under-uncertainty）策略，BOBa自适应地将代理模型推理和昂贵评估集中在经验上有希望的分区上，同时保持原则性的探索。该方法避免了全库推理，实现了计算资源的动态分配。

Result: 在真实合成按需（synthesis-on-demand）化合物库上的实验表明：1）基于乐观不确定性的多臂老虎机算法与有意义的动作空间分区相结合，对于有效分配推理和评估资源至关重要；2）BOBa能够实现筛选性能与代理推理成本之间的可调节权衡，支持在当前规模库上进行实际优化。

Conclusion: BOBa为超大规模库的虚拟筛选提供了一条可行途径，通过消除全库推理需求，使得在现有数十亿至万亿级化合物库上进行实际优化成为可能。该方法建立了一种可调节的性能-成本权衡机制，为未来更大型分子库的筛选奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Identifying high-utility candidates from massive discrete spaces under expensive evaluations is a recurring challenge across the sciences, with structure-based drug discovery as a prominent example. While surrogate-based optimization can increase sample efficiency by reducing the number of expensive evaluations, modern molecular libraries have reached billions to trillions of compounds, making full-library surrogate inference itself a major computational bottleneck. We introduce BOBa, a bandit-guided surrogate optimization framework that eliminates full-library inference by adaptively allocating computation across partitions of the action space. By treating partitions as arms in a multi-armed bandit, BOBa concentrates inference and evaluations on empirically promising partitions while maintaining principled exploration. Experiments on real-world synthesis-on-demand libraries demonstrate that optimism-under-uncertainty bandits, combined with meaningful action space partitioning, are essential for effective allocation of inference and evaluations. Our findings reveal a tunable tradeoff between screening performance and surrogate inference cost, which supports practical optimization over current libraries, and establishes a viable route to ultra-large library virtual screening.

</details>


### [51] [Zero-Shot Size Transfer for Neural ODEs on Sparse Random Graphs: Graphon Limits and Adjoint Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26662)
*Mingsong Yan,Zhida Wang,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究图神经微分方程(GNDEs)的零样本尺寸迁移原理，建立了稀疏随机图上的定量理论，证明了GNDE解向图极限(图ON-NDE)的收敛速率，分析了离散-优化与优化-离散训练的一致性，并通过实验验证了理论结果和迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNDE的局部、尺寸无关滤波器暗示了零样本尺寸迁移原理(在小图上训练，直接部署到大图)，但缺乏严格的定量理论支撑。

Method: 1) 将图ON神经微分方程(Graphon-NDE)和对偶Graphon-NDE定义为前向和对偶GNDE系统的无限节点极限，证明适定性；2) 对稀疏随机图($n$节点，稀疏参数$\alpha_n$)证明GNDE解以$O((\alpha_n n)^{-1/2})$速率(含对数因子)收敛到Graphon-NDE解；3) 建立对偶系统中隐状态和参数梯度的均匀时间收敛界；4) 分析显式欧拉离散下DTO和OTD训练的一致性，给出$O(1/M)$和$O(1/M^2)$的差异阶。

Result: 理论收敛速率在HSBM和tent graphon上的实验得到验证，零样本迁移实验在四类图ON上展示了学习到的GNDE可准确部署到更大独立采样图上。

Conclusion: 建立了GNDE零样本尺寸迁移的严格定量理论，证明了稀疏随机图上的收敛速率和训练一致性，为GNDE的实际大图部署提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Graph Neural Differential Equations (GNDEs) model continuous-time graph dynamics by parameterizing Neural ODE velocity fields with Graph Neural Networks. Their local, size-independent filters suggest a zero-shot size-transfer principle: train on a small graph and deploy on larger, similar graphs without retraining. We develop a quantitative theory for this principle on sparse random graphs sampled from graphons. We consider Graphon Neural Differential Equations (Graphon-NDEs) and adjoint Graphon-NDEs as the infinite-node limits of the forward and adjoint GNDE systems, and establish well-posedness. For an $n$-node random graph with sparsity parameter $α_n$, we prove trajectory-wise convergence of GNDE solutions to Graphon-NDE solutions at rate $O((α_n n)^{-1/2})$, up to logarithmic factors, with high probability. We also establish uniform-in-time convergence bounds for adjoint systems governing hidden-state and parameter gradients. We further study discretize-then-optimize (DTO) and optimize-then-discretize (OTD) training. Under explicit Euler discretization with $M$ steps, we show that DTO and OTD are asymptotically consistent, with hidden-state and local parameter-gradient discrepancies of orders $O(1/M)$ and $O(1/M^2)$, respectively, up to sparsity and logarithmic factors. Experiments on HSBM and tent graphons support the theoretical rates, while zero-shot transfer experiments across four graphon classes demonstrate accurate deployment of learned GNDEs on larger independently sampled graphs.

</details>


### [52] [PersistentKV: Page-Aware Decode Scheduling for Long-Context LLM Serving on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26666)
*Muhammad Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PersistentKV提出了一种原生块表解码注意力引擎和页面感知调度策略，通过按KV头分组映射工作、重用K/V tile、支持原生页表及紧凑工作队列调度，在长上下文和混合序列长度场景下相比FlashInfer实现1.063-1.399x的端到端吞吐提升，证明了工作分配而非仅注意力计算是决定性服务系统变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型服务中，KV缓存移动日益成为瓶颈而非稠密矩阵乘法。现有paged-attention系统（如FlashInfer）的单核实现最优，但在低活跃长上下文解码时GPU利用率不足，混合序列长度下精确长度启动与粗粒度填充批处理之间存在矛盾，亟需页面感知的调度策略。

Method: 1）设计原生块表解码注意力引擎，按KV头分组映射工作，重用K/V tile；2）支持原生页表；3）引入紧凑工作队列调度，仅执行非空的行-KV头-序列拆分任务；4）构建校准自适应策略，根据活跃批次大小在FlashInfer与PersistentKV间切换。

Result: 在RTX 3060上，FP16、页大小16、Hq=32、Hkv=8、d=128条件下：B8双峰/均匀/Zipf负载吞吐提升1.063-1.265x，B1分桶负载提升1.399x；B4双峰边界案例自适应策略避免回归，选择FlashInfer。

Conclusion: 工作分配而非仅注意力计算是关键的系统变量，自适应页面感知解码调度在实际服务场景中存在明确的系统生态位。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model (LLM) serving is increasingly limited by key-value (KV) cache movement rather than dense matrix multiplication. Modern paged-attention systems reduce KV-cache fragmentation and mature kernels such as FlashInfer provide highly optimized native-paged decode attention. However, the best single-kernel implementation is not always the best serving schedule: low-active long-context decode can under-utilize commodity GPUs, while mixed sequence lengths introduce a tension between many exact-length launches and coarse padded batches. We present PersistentKV, a native block-table decode attention engine and page-aware scheduling study for grouped-query attention (GQA). PersistentKV maps work by KV-head group, is designed to reuse K,V tiles across grouped query heads, supports native page tables, and adds a compact workqueue schedule that executes only non-empty row-KV-head-sequence-split tasks. On an RTX 3060 with FP16, page size 16, Hq=32, Hkv=8, d=128, and identical correctness tolerance against FlashInfer, a calibrated adaptive policy selects FlashInfer for small active batches, PersistentKV sequence splitting for B1 long-context steps, and PersistentKV workqueue scheduling for B8 long-context steps. With thresholds and split counts fixed on calibration traces, one held-out trace seed improves synchronized wall throughput by 1.063-1.265x on B8 bimodal, uniform, and Zipf-like workloads and by 1.399x on a B1 bucketed trace. On the B4 bimodal boundary case, the policy avoids the PersistentKV regression by selecting FlashInfer. These results identify a concrete systems niche for adaptive page-aware decode scheduling and show that work assignment, not only attention math, is a decisive serving-system variable.

</details>


### [53] [Algorithmic Foundations of Deep Learning: Complexity-Theoretic Rates and a Characterization of Universal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26705)
*Anastasis Kratsios,Simone Brugiapaglia,Bum Jun Kim,Gregory Cousins,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经网络不仅应被视为灵活基函数，更应被视为计算模型。证明了若函数可通过基本门语言的可计算实值电路实现，则神经网络能以显式深度、宽度和非零参数界限逼近该函数。神经网络复杂度由算法复杂度而非仅正则性决定。任何满足并行化条件的可定义神经网络模型，当且仅当包含非仿射非线性时才是通用逼近器。通过多个案例验证了理论的范围和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上通过最优基展开方案研究前馈神经网络的表达能力，但该视角不完整：它主要通过正则性来刻画复杂度，无法区分具有相似正则性但直觉上简单与复杂的对象（如平方根函数与典型布朗路径）。作者旨在建立神经网络作为计算模型的理论框架。

Method: 将神经网络视为实值电路，建立从可计算电路到神经网络的可编译性定理，给出显式的深度、宽度和非零参数界限。提出可定义神经网络模型的并行化条件，证明其通用逼近性的充要条件。通过具体案例（连续函数、Besov类、全纯函数、数值算法、最短路径计算）展示理论适用范围和精度。

Result: 神经网络复杂度由算法复杂度而非仅正则性决定；任何满足并行化条件的可定义NN模型（允许多变量非线性如注意力或层归一化）是通用逼近器当且仅当包含非仿射非线性。在k顶点图最短路径计算中，编译热带动态规划电路得到的NN仅需O(log(1/ε))非零参数，远优于通用Lipschitz逼近的O(ε^{-c k^2})。

Conclusion: 神经网络表达能力不仅取决于正则性，还取决于算法的计算复杂度。通过将神经网络视为计算模型，可以解释和获得更高效、更精确的逼近结果，特别是对于具有算法结构的问题。

Abstract: Feedforward neural network (NN) expressivity is typically studied by emulating optimal basis-expansion schemes. While powerful, this perspective is incomplete: it primarily captures complexity through regularity, and therefore does not distinguish intuitively simple and complicated objects with comparable regularity, such as the square-root function and a typical Brownian path.
  The guiding message is that neural networks should be viewed not only as flexible basis functions, but also as models of computation. If a function is computable by a real-valued circuit over a prescribed elementary gate language, then it can be computed to comparable accuracy by an NN with explicit depth, width, and non-zero-parameter bounds controlled by the depth, width, gate count, and gate structure. Thus, neural-network complexity is not governed by regularity alone, but also by algorithmic complexity. We then show that any definable NN model satisfying a natural parallelization condition, allowing possibly multivariate non-linearities such as attention or layer normalization, is a universal approximator if and only if it contains a non-affine nonlinearity.
  The scope of our theory is illustrated by deducing universal approximation guarantees for continuous functions, minimax-optimal approximation guarantees for Besov classes, logarithmic-error complexity for holomorphic functions, and by showing that NNs can emulate numerical algorithms such as Newton-Raphson root finding and power iteration without architecture-specific arguments. Its precision is illustrated by shortest-path computation on $k$-vertex graphs: compiling the tropical dynamic-programming circuit yields NNs with O(log(1/ε)) non-zero parameters, exponentially improving in 1/ε over the generic $O(ε^{-c k^2})$ Lipschitz-approximation scale, for a constant c>0.

</details>


### [54] [HyperDFlash: MHC-Aligned Block Speculative Decoding with Gated Residual Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26744)
*Luxi Lin,Shuang Peng,Rui Ma,Junhao Hua,Shuwei Fan,Zhengda Qin,Qiang Wang,Hongjian Sun,Fangmin Chen,Songwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyperDFlash是一种面向DeepSeek-V4多超连接(MHC)架构的块并行推测解码框架，通过预折叠残差状态、门控残差缩减器和KL蒸馏损失等优化，解决了原生MTP模块后期草案精度下降及DFlash无法适配MHC架构的问题，在数学推理、代码合成和对话基准上实现了更优的接受草案长度和解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek-V4原生MTP模块在早期位置草案生成表现良好，但由于未验证中间token的误差累积，后续位置的草案精度严重下降，影响接受率。同时，原有的DFlash方法无法直接适配MHC架构，因为DeepSeek-V4的多路径残差流与常规草案设计存在特征不匹配问题。

Method: 提出三种模型对齐优化：(1) 采用预折叠残差状态作为唯一条件信号，保留多路径结构信息；(2) 用轻量级门控残差缩减器替代重型线性压缩器，从超连接头继承参数，实现输入感知的路径聚合，参数减少三个数量级；(3) 在LM头部应用针对性的KL蒸馏损失，利用完整目标概率分布进行正则化，提升早期训练阶段的草案质量。

Result: 在数学推理、代码合成和对话基准测试中，HyperDFlash在平均接受草案长度和解码加速方面持续优于原生MTP基线和原始DFlash适配方案。

Conclusion: 验证了MHC对齐、门控缩减和针对性蒸馏对高性能推测解码的有效性，表明HyperDFlash能够有效解决MHC架构下的特征不匹配和草案精度退化问题。

Abstract: We present HyperDFlash, a block-parallel speculative decoding framework tailored to the novel multi-hyper-connection (MHC) architecture proposed by DeepSeek-V4. Despite the strong initial-token drafting performance of the native Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) module in DeepSeek-V4, its draft accuracy degrades sharply at later positions, as error accumulation from unverified intermediate tokens harms acceptance rates. Although the original DFlash method supports efficient one-pass block drafting, it cannot be seamlessly adapted to the MHC paradigm, since the multi-path residual stream of DeepSeek-V4 induces feature misalignment with conventional drafting designs. To resolve this mismatch, we propose two model-aligned optimizations for MHC residual streams. First, we adopt pre-collapse residual states as the exclusive conditioning signal, preserving multi-path structural information and aligning the drafter with the native prediction pathway of the target model. Second, we replace the heavy generic linear compressor with a lightweight gated residual reducer, whose parameters are inherited from the built-in hyper-connection head. This design yields input-aware path aggregation with three orders of magnitude fewer parameters while maintaining architectural alignment. We further enhance training via a targeted KL distillation loss applied to the LM-head, which regularizes predictions against the full target probability distribution and improves draft quality at early training stages. Experiments across math reasoning, code synthesis, and conversational benchmarks show that HyperDFlash consistently outperforms both the native MTP baseline and vanilla DFlash adaptation. It achieves substantial gains in average accepted draft length and decoding speedup, validating the effectiveness of MHC alignment, gated reduction, and targeted distillation for high-performance speculative decoding.

</details>


### [55] [Structure Before Collapse: Transient semantic geometry in next-token prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26749)
*Yize Zhao,Isabel Papadimitriou,Christos Thrampoulidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 尽管语言模型使用独热标签（one-hot labels）进行下一个词预测训练，但模型仍然能学习到潜在的语义结构。本文通过三个合成控制实验发现，语义几何结构在训练早期出现，但最终会收敛到对称的神经坍缩（Neural Collapse）状态。研究通过Gram矩阵分析这种相变，并对无约束特征模型进行了初步改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经坍缩理论预测，在平衡独热分类任务中，模型表示会等距分离，忽略输入中的语义相似性。但语言模型在独热标签训练下却能学到潜在的语义结构，这一矛盾激发了作者探索梯度下降如何在稀疏的共现统计中仍然发现语义结构。

Method: 设计了三个合成控制实验，其中输入具有潜在语义因子但映射到不同的独热标签；通过分析训练过程中的表示演化、Gram矩阵分析，并对无约束特征模型（UFM）进行修改来捕捉早期语义几何。

Result: 1）语义几何在训练早期出现，表示按共享属性聚类；2）这种结构是暂时的，足够容量和训练时间后模型会达到对称的神经坍缩状态；3）通过Gram矩阵分析研究了这一相变过程。

Conclusion: 揭示了语言模型在独热标签训练下语义结构涌现与神经坍缩之间的动态竞争关系，提出了对无约束特征模型的初步修改以描述这种早期语义几何。

Abstract: Neural Collapse predicts that balanced one-hot classification pushes model representations to be equally far from each other; a symmetric configuration that depends only on the output label and ignores any semantic similarity in the inputs. This creates a puzzle: next-token prediction language models are trained predominantly (as context length increases) with one-hot labels: the same context is very unlikely to appear twice in training with different labels. However, they clearly learn latent structural features. That is, despite the one-hot training regime, a language model's contextual embeddings represent the fact that the next word in ''Mary broke the ___'' is likely to be filled by tokens in the latent classes of a) medium-sized, b) rigid, c) inanimate nouns. How does gradient descent find such categorical semantic structure when co-occurrence statistics collapse to one-hot sparsity, eliminating any shared next-tokens among different contexts? To investigate this tension we identify three synthetic controlled settings where inputs have latent semantic factors but are mapped to distinct one-hot labels. We find that semantic geometry emerges early in training, and that representations cluster by shared attributes despite receiving no explicit supervision to do so. This structure is transient: with sufficient capacity and time, the model eventually reaches the predicted symmetric state where all representations are equally separated. We study this phase transition through Gram matrix analysis and propose a preliminary modification to the commonly used unconstrained features model to capture the emergent semantic geometry.

</details>


### [56] [Batch-Invariant Spectral Intelligence for Robust and Explainable Insect Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26757)
*Majharulislam Babor,Giacomo Rossi,Annalisa Altavilla,Oliver Schlüter,Marina M. -C. Höhne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Batch-Invariant Spectral Network (BISN)，一种结合可学习预处理模块和熵正则化对抗目标的端到端框架，用于抑制近红外光谱中的批次效应，在三种食用昆虫物种认证任务中，留一批次交叉验证平均准确率达0.93，超越最强基线4%，并具备生化可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食用昆虫作为替代蛋白质来源需要可靠的物种认证方法以控制过敏原暴露、防止掺假并满足监管要求。近红外光谱虽提供快速分析工具，但生产批次间的光谱测量差异导致模型在未见批次上性能下降，亟需一种能够消除批次效应的方法。

Method: 提出Batch-Invariant Spectral Network (BISN)，包含：(1) 以Savitzky-Golay滤波初始化的可学习预处理模块；(2) 熵正则化对抗目标，在特征提取前抑制批次特异性光谱变化，区别于传统领域对抗神经网络仅在特征提取后执行域适应。

Result: 使用三个物种（Acheta domesticus, Hermetia illucens, Tenebrio molitor）共2700条光谱，来自三个独立生产批次，留一批次交叉验证平均准确率0.93（标准差0.04），超越最强基线4%。可解释AI分析确认模型决策始终依赖脂质和蛋白质吸收区域。

Conclusion: BISN在工业现实条件下实现了跨批次鲁棒性和生化可解释性，为自动化昆虫物种认证提供了有效方案。代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Edible insects offer an efficient source of alternative protein, requiring less land, water and emitting less greenhouse gas than conventional livestock. However, their successful integration into the food supply chain demands reliable species authentication to control allergen exposure, prevent adulteration, and meet regulatory standards. Near-infrared spectroscopy provides a rapid analytical tool, but its performance drops when applied to production batches unseen during training due to batch-to-batch variation in spectral measurements. We introduce the Batch-Invariant Spectral Network (BISN), an end-to-end framework that combines a learnable preprocessing module, initialised with Savitzky-Golay filtering, with an entropy-regularised adversarial objective to suppress batch-specific spectral variation. In contrast to Domain-Adversarial Neural Networks, which enforce domain adaptation only after feature extraction, BISN suppress batch-effects before species-specific features are learned. Using 2,700 spectra from three species (Acheta domesticus, Hermetia illucens, and Tenebrio molitor) collected across three independent production batches, BISN achieves a mean leave-one-batch-out accuracy of 0.93 (standard deviation 0.04), outperforming the strongest baseline by four percent. Further insights gained by using explainable AI confirm that model decisions consistently rely on the lipid and protein absorption regions across all folds, connecting predictive performance to known insect biochemistry. BISN addresses both cross-batch robustness and biochemical interpretability for automated insect species authentication under realistic industrial conditions. The source code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/majharB/bisn.

</details>


### [57] [Escaping Iterative Parameter-Space Noise: Differentially Private Learning with a Hypernetwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26772)
*Naoki Nishikawa,Shokichi Takakura,Satoshi Hasegawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的差分隐私学习框架，通过超网络在公共数据集上训练，将私有数据集的低维嵌入聚合加噪后生成目标模型参数，避免DP-SGD的迭代参数空间噪声注入，显著降低噪声影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP-SGD等基于梯度的方法需要在训练过程中反复向高维参数空间注入大量噪声，严重影响了模型效用。本文旨在设计一种避免迭代参数空间优化的DP学习框架，以降低隐私噪声带来的负面影响。

Method: 使用超网络在公共数据集上训练；将每个私有示例嵌入到低维表示中；聚合嵌入并添加扰动得到DP数据集嵌入；超网络从含噪嵌入生成目标模型参数。隐私噪声仅在低维数据集表示上注入一次。

Result: 在合成环境中理论证明，在固定隐私预算下，该方法比DP-SGD获得更高效用；在扩散模型的LoRA微调中，比DP-SGD和其他公共数据引导方法取得更低的FID。

Conclusion: 通过将隐私噪声注入从高维参数空间的多次迭代转移至低维嵌入的一次性注入，该框架能有效降低噪声影响，提升DP训练的模型效用。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) training of neural networks is often hindered by the large amount of noise required by gradient-based methods such as DP-SGD, which repeatedly inject high-dimensional noise in parameter space throughout training. In this paper, we propose a new framework for DP learning that avoids iterative optimization in parameter space. Instead of updating the target model using privatized gradients, we employ a hypernetwork trained on public datasets to map a private dataset to the parameters of the target model. Specifically, each example is embedded into a low-dimensional representation, the embeddings are aggregated and perturbed to obtain a DP dataset embedding, and the hypernetwork generates the target model parameters from this noisy embedding. Because privacy noise is injected only once into a low-dimensional dataset representation, our approach can significantly reduce the adverse effect of noise. We theoretically show in a synthetic setting that, under a fixed privacy budget, models produced by our approach achieve higher utility than those trained with DP-SGD. Moreover, we apply our approach to LoRA fine-tuning of diffusion models and show that it achieves lower FID than LoRA models trained with DP-SGD and other public-data-guided methods.

</details>


### [58] [Reproducibility Study of "AlphaEdit: Null-Space Constrained Knowledge Editing for Language Models"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26783)
*Ananth K S,Arya Hariharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了AlphaEdit（一种基于零空间约束投影的知识编辑方法），发现其在原始设置下结果可复现，但存在流畅性和一致性指标的差异。扩展实验表明：该方法优势不泛化到新模型架构（源于定位-编辑范式的架构假设被违反），大规模序列编辑下性能会退化（零空间投影的保护是有界的而非无条件的），且大规模编辑会损害下游任务能力和安全相关的拒绝行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对Fang等人（2025）提出的AlphaEdit方法进行可重复性研究，验证其在原始实验条件下的结果是否可复现，并进一步评估该方法在更广泛场景下的泛化能力与局限性。

Method: 1）在原始实验设置下复现AlphaEdit在LLaMA3、GPT2-XL、GPT-J上的结果；2）扩展至新模型架构（验证泛化性）；3）延长序列编辑长度（压力测试）；4）在BoolQ、HellaSwag、XSTest三个额外基准上评估编辑后模型的下游任务能力和安全行为。

Result: 1）成功复现原始指标，但发现流畅性和一致性指标存在差异；2）AlphaEdit的优势在新模型系列上不统一泛化，原因在于定位-编辑范式的架构假设被违反；3）序列编辑数量大幅增加时，性能会退化，零空间投影的保护是有界的而非无条件的；4）大规模序列编辑会降低一般下游任务能力和安全相关的拒绝行为。

Conclusion: AlphaEdit在原始范围内表现如报告所述，但其核心理论保证对模型架构和编辑规模敏感，这对其实际部署具有实际意义。

Abstract: Fang et al. (2025) introduced a null-space constrained projection, named AlphaEdit, for locate-then-edit knowledge editing methods, theoretically guaranteeing that edits do not disrupt previously preserved knowledge, and reports substantial gains over existing editing methods on LLaMA3, GPT2-XL, and GPT-J. In this work, we present a reproducibility study of AlphaEdit, reproducing its reported results under the original experimental setup and extending the evaluation along three axes: new model architectures, additional downstream benchmarks, and substantially longer sequential editing horizons. We successfully reproduce AlphaEdit's reported metrics across the original models, though we identify a discrepancy in the reported fluency and consistency metric. Extending AlphaEdit to newer model families, we find that its advantage does not generalize uniformly, which we trace to architectural assumptions in the locate-then-edit paradigm that are violated by these newer models. We further stress-test AlphaEdit's central sequential-editing claim by extending the number of edits well beyond those evaluated in the original paper, and find that performance, which is stable at the originally reported scale, degrades as edits reach a much higher count, indicating that the null-space projection's protection against catastrophic forgetting is bounded rather than unconditional. Finally, we extend evaluation of edited models on three extra benchmarks, namely, BoolQ, HellaSwag, and XSTest, and we find that large-scale sequential editing degrades both general downstream task competence and safety-relevant refusal behavior. Our results confirm that AlphaEdit performs as reported within its original scope, while showing that its core theoretical guarantees are sensitive to model architecture and editing scale in ways that have practical implications for its deployment.

</details>


### [59] [AIGP: An LLM-Based Framework for Long-Term Value Alignment in E-Commerce Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26787)
*Chennan Ma,Yanning Zhang,Siqi Hong,Xiuchong Wang,Fei Xiao,Keping Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIGP框架，利用大语言模型结合领域知识与结构化/文本数据进行可解释的动态定价，通过离线强化学习训练的长期价值估计器（LTVE）作为奖励模型，结合直接偏好优化（DPO）对齐长期业务目标，在淘宝工厂大规模实验中实现GMV+13.21%、ROI+7.59%、里程碑达成率+8.20%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大规模电商动态定价模型存在可解释性差、非结构化信息利用不足、与长期业务目标（如累计GMV、ROI、里程碑达成）不一致等问题。

Method: 提出AIGP框架：①利用LLM注入领域知识、结构化数据和文本上下文进行可解释定价；②采用监督微调进行知识蒸馏以实现高效部署；③核心组件LTVE通过离线强化学习训练，作为奖励模型对候选定价动作评分并选择偏好对，通过DPO对齐定价策略与长期业务目标。

Result: 在淘宝工厂的离线评估和线上A/B测试中，相较于生产基线，AIGP在14天内实现了GMV提升13.21%、ROI提升7.59%、里程碑达成率提升8.20%，同时提供可解释的定价理由。

Conclusion: AIGP框架有效解决了传统定价模型的可解释性与长期目标对齐问题，验证了LLM结合离线强化学习与DPO在大规模电商动态定价中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Traditional dynamic pricing models in large-scale e-commerce suffer from limited interpretability, poor utilization of unstructured information, and misalignment with long-term business objectives such as cumulative Gross Merchandise Value (GMV), Return on Investment (ROI) and milestone achievement. We propose AIGP, a novel framework that leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) prompted with domain knowledge, structured data and textual context to make interpretable, knowledge-aware pricing decisions. For efficient deployment while maintaining high-quality outputs, we employ supervised fine-tuning for knowledge distillation. Central to AIGP is the Long-Term Value Estimator (LTVE), trained via offline reinforcement learning on historical data, which serves as a reward model to score candidate pricing actions and select preference pairs for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), thereby aligning the pricing policy with long-term business objectives. Extensive offline evaluations and large-scale online A/B tests on Tao Factory demonstrate that AIGP achieves significant improvements: +13.21% in GMV, +7.59% in ROI, and +8.20% in milestone achievement rate over 14 days compared to the production baseline, while simultaneously providing interpretable and transparent pricing rationales.

</details>


### [60] [Reasoning Quality Emerges Early: Data Curation for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26797)
*Hongyi Henry Jin,Wenhan Yang,Meysam Ghaffari,Carlos Morato,Baharan Mirzasoleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅利用初始推理token（前100-1000个token）即可高效筛选高质量SFT数据的方法，通过随机扰动检查点的损失值检测困难样本，并在多个数据集上验证了其有效性，相比现有方法提升1.7%性能且节省91%的token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT数据筛选方法依赖强推理模型基于多样性和难度进行过滤，成本高昂且数据质量次优。

Method: 利用预训练模型随机扰动检查点上前100个推理token的损失值检测困难问题；通过前1k推理token在多个扰动检查点上的损失模式相似性来识别梯度相似的示例。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama3.1-8B模型上，在M23K医疗推理和OpenThoughts-Math数据集上，性能提升高达1.7%，token效率提升91%。

Conclusion: 仅利用初始推理token的损失特征即可高效筛选高质量SFT数据，极大地降低了数据筛选成本并提升了效率。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a small, high-quality set of long reasoning traces is an effective approach for eliciting strong reasoning capabilities in Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods for curating high-quality SFT data rely heavily on strong reasoning models to filter examples based on diversity and difficulty, making the curation process costly while often yielding suboptimal data quality. In this work, we show that diverse and challenging reasoning examples can be identified using only the initial reasoning tokens. Specifically, we demonstrate that difficult problems can be reliably detected based on the loss of the first 100 reasoning tokens evaluated at a randomly perturbed checkpoint of the pretrained model. We further show that examples exhibiting similar loss patterns over their first 1k reasoning tokens across a small number of perturbed checkpoints extrapolating along the fine-tuning trajectory provably induce similar gradients. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on fine-tuning Qwen2.5-7B and Llama3.1-8B models on the M23K medical reasoning and OpenThoughts-Math datasets. Our method outperforms existing baselines by up to 1.7% while being 91% more token efficient.

</details>


### [61] [Quantization in Federated Learning: Methods, Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26822)
*Farwa Ikram,Dipanwita Thakur,Antonella Guzzo,Giancarlo Fortino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从联邦学习（FL）视角系统综述了量化技术，提出了围绕FL特有维度（如客户端异构性、聚合一致性、通信调度适应、非IID鲁棒性、隐私/安全集成、硬件/能耗协同优化）的新型分类体系，深入分析了量化与客户端漂移、部分参与、收敛稳定性、安全聚合、差分隐私等核心FL行为的交互作用，并给出了面向移动、IoT和边缘平台的部署设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习的可扩展性受限于通信瓶颈、设备异构性和非IID数据挑战，量化是缓解这些限制的有效机制，但缺乏从FL视角出发的系统性综述。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，首次提出面向FL特有维度的量化技术分类体系，并交叉分析量化与FL核心行为（客户端漂移、部分参与、收敛稳定性、安全聚合、差分隐私）的交互关系。

Result: 构建了涵盖客户端异构性、聚合一致性、通信调度适应、非IID鲁棒性、隐私/安全集成、硬件/能耗协同优化六个维度的量化分类框架，并识别了跨方法洞察、研究空白与设计指南。

Conclusion: 量化不仅是压缩技术，更是影响现代FL性能、鲁棒性和实用性的基础系统组件，本文为移动、IoT和边缘平台上的量化FL实践提供了系统化指导。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has become a foundational paradigm for privacy-preserving distributed intelligence, yet its scalability remains fundamentally constrained by communication bottlenecks, device heterogeneity, and the challenges of training under statistically non-IID data. Quantization is one of the most effective mechanisms for mitigating these limitations, reducing both uplink/downlink payloads and on-device computation. This paper provides the first FL-centric systematic review of quantization, introducing a novel taxonomy organized around FL-specific dimensions, including client heterogeneity, aggregation consistency, communication-scheduling adaptation, non-IID robustness, privacy/security integration, and hardware/energy co-optimization. Beyond cataloging existing methods, we analyze how quantization interacts with core FL behaviors such as client drift, partial participation, convergence stability, secure aggregation, and differential privacy. We further identify cross-method insights, open research gaps, and design guidelines for practitioners deploying quantized FL on mobile, IoT, and edge platforms. This survey thus establishes quantization not merely as a compression technique, but as a fundamental systems component shaping the performance, robustness, and practicality of modern FL.

</details>


### [62] [Asymptotically Optimal Learning for Parametric Prophet Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26893)
*Jung-hun Kim,Anna Grebennikova,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了指数型参数族（含指数分布、Pareto分布、有界支撑幂族分布）下先知不等式问题的在线学习。刻画了完全信息下的最优渐近竞争比：无界支撑情形为 $(θ/(θ-c_+))^{c_+/θ} / Γ(1-c_+/θ)$，有界支撑情形为1。提出了基于置信度的动态规划在线学习策略，无需离线样本即可达到相同最优渐近竞争比，并给出了典范分布的收敛速率与数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为指数型参数族下的先知不等式问题提供最优渐近竞争比的理论刻画，并设计无需离线样本、仅依赖在线观测即可达到该最优竞争比的实用学习算法。

Method: 理论分析完全信息最优渐近竞争比；提出基于置信度的动态规划（confidence-based dynamic programming）在线学习策略，利用显式参数结构进行在线推断。

Result: 无界支撑情形最优渐近竞争比为 $(θ/(θ-c_+))^{c_+/θ} / Γ(1-c_+/θ)$；有界支撑情形为1。所提在线策略达到相同最优竞争比，并给出了典范分布的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 通过利用参数族的显式结构，可以在无离线样本的条件下实现与完全信息情形相同的渐近最优竞争比，理论结果与数值实验均验证了算法的有效性。

Abstract: We study learning in prophet inequalities with i.i.d. rewards drawn from an exponential-type parametric family with an unknown parameter $θ$, a class that includes exponential, Pareto, and bounded-support power-family distributions. We first characterize the optimal full-information asymptotic competitive ratio for this family. In the unbounded-support case, the limit is $ {\left(θ/({θ-c_+})\right)^{c_+/θ}}/ {Γ(1-c_+/θ)},$ while in the bounded-support case, the limit is $1$. We then propose a confidence-based dynamic-programming policy for online learning. By exploiting the explicit parametric structure, the policy achieves the same optimal asymptotic competitive ratio using only online observations, without external offline samples. We further derive distribution-specific convergence rates for canonical examples. Finally, numerical experiments on synthetic instances illustrate the performance of our algorithm.

</details>


### [63] [GEOALIGN: Geometric Rollout Curation for Robust LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26917)
*Ting Zhou,Zhenqing Ling,Yiyang Zhao,Ying Shen,Daoyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Geoalign是一种轻量级插件方法，通过检测和修正在线强化学习中轨迹的方向不一致性（directional inconsistency），稳定大语言模型的对齐训练，在对话对齐和数学推理任务上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习在使用噪声或错误指定的奖励信号对齐LLMs时训练不稳定，作者识别出一种称为"方向不一致性"的失败模式：批次内少数高奖励轨迹的表示空间偏好方向与批次多数严重冲突，导致高方差和破坏性更新。

Method: Geoalign包含三个步骤：(i) 在提示内形成偏好对；(ii) 学习在线投影器，基于每个轨迹的隐藏状态集中奖励排序的位移方向；(iii) 通过角度偏差检测与批次共识原型方向不一致的轨迹，并用同一提示内的稳定替代轨迹进行修正。该方法仅需前向传播，计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在对话对齐（使用学习到的奖励模型）和数学推理（使用二值验证奖励）任务上，Geoalign提升了最终性能并减少了训练振荡，优于PF-PPO、PAR、PODS和Seed-GRPO等基线方法。

Conclusion: 潜在的方向共识可以作为在线LLM强化学习中有效的可靠性信号，Geoalign通过检测和修正方向不一致性来稳定训练过程。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning is widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with reward signals, yet training can be unstable under noisy or misspecified rewards. We identify a failure mode we call directional inconsistency: within a batch, a small set of high-reward rollouts induces representation-space preference directions that sharply disagree with the batch majority, resulting in high-variance and destabilizing updates. We propose geoalign, a lightweight plug-in for rollout curation in iterative policy optimization. Geoalign (i) forms within-prompt preference pairs, (ii) learns an online projector on per-rollout hidden states to concentrate reward-ordered displacement directions, and (iii) detects directionally inconsistent rollouts via their angular deviation from a batch consensus prototype and rectifies them with within-prompt stable alternatives. Geoalign is forward-pass only and adds negligible overhead. Across dialogue alignment with a learned reward model and mathematical reasoning with binary verified rewards, Geoalign improves final performance and reduces training oscillation, outperforming PF-PPO, PAR, PODS, and Seed-GRPO. These results suggest latent directional consensus as an effective reliability signal for online LLM RL.

</details>


### [64] [Decision-Aligned Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26990)
*Annika Schneider,Tommy Rochussen,Joshua Stiller,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前机器学习中的不确定性估计评估指标（如负对数似然、期望校准误差）与下游决策效用之间存在错位，提出"决策对齐"准则，并引入先验加权效用度量作为修正方案，实验证明新指标能与实际决策效用保持一致，而传统指标则不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性估计评估指标（如NLL、ECE）虽然被广泛使用，但它们在评估中表现良好并不保证在下游决策任务中具有实际效用，存在评估与决策目标之间的错位问题。

Method: 提出"决策对齐"准则用于判断评估指标是否与下游效用对齐；在此基础上，提出先验加权效用度量（prior-weighted utility metrics），作为一类特殊的proper scoring规则，实现决策对齐的不确定性评估。

Result: 在基准实验和真实案例研究中，提出的先验加权效用度量始终与实际的决策效用保持一致，而传统指标（NLL、ECE等）则无法做到这一点。

Conclusion: 当前不确定性评估协议存在缺陷，本文提供了一种原则性的扩展方法，使评估指标能够面向决策相关性，弥补了传统指标在决策效用对齐方面的不足。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimates in machine learning are typically evaluated using generic metrics such as the negative log-likelihood and expected calibration error, yet good performance on such metrics does not necessarily imply high utility in downstream decisions. We introduce decision-alignment, a criterion that reveals which evaluation metrics meaningfully align with downstream utilities. Applying this framework, we show that many widely used uncertainty metrics are either misaligned with common decision problems or encode pathological prior beliefs about the downstream task. We then propose prior-weighted utility metrics, a special class of proper scoring rules that provides decision-aligned uncertainty evaluation. Across benchmark experiments and real-world case studies, our metrics consistently align with realized decision utility, while conventional metrics do not. Our results surface flaws in the current UQ evaluation protocol and offer a principled extension of existing metrics toward decision-relevant UQ evaluation.

</details>


### [65] [Uncertainty quantification via conformal prediction in data assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27001)
*Catherine George,Alireza Javanmardi,Tijana Janjić,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索了共形预测（CP）这一机器学习方法在数值天气预报中量化不确定性的适用性，通过一维修正浅水模型对比了三种CP变体与传统的基于集合的不确定性方法，并探讨了CP不确定性在数据同化循环中的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化不确定性演化对数值天气预报中的概率预测和数据同化至关重要，传统基于集合的方法存在计算成本高等局限，因此需要探索新的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 使用一维修正浅水模型模拟对流过程，比较了三种CP变体（标准CP、归一化CP、共形分位数回归）与传统的标准差区间和集合离散度，评估指标包括平均经验覆盖、平均区间长度、漏低、漏高和平均区间得分损失（AISL），并探索了CP不确定性在数据同化循环中的集成。

Result: 揭示了每种方法在简化大气模型中量化不确定性的优势和局限性，CP方法能够以选定的置信水平提供可能的预报结果集合。

Conclusion: CP作为传统基于集合的不确定性量化的补充方法在简化大气模型中具有有效性，但不同CP变体各有其适用场景和局限。

Abstract: Quantifying the evolution of uncertainty is critical to both probabilistic forecasting and data assimilation in numerical weather prediction. In this study, we investigate the applicability of conformal prediction (CP), a recent machine learning (ML) method, to quantify uncertainty in a controlled, idealized setting. We use the one dimensional modified shallow water model, designed to mimic the convective process. CP provides a set of possible outcomes with a chosen confidence level. Here, we compare and evaluate the average empirical coverage, the average interval length, miss low, miss high and average interval score loss (AISL) for three variants of CP, namely a) Standard CP, b) Normalized CP and c) Conformalized Quantile Regression. We further compare these CP-based uncertainty estimates with traditional ensemble-based measures such as standard deviation intervals and ensemble spread. In addition, we investigate the integration of CP-derived uncertainty within the data assimilation cycle through CP perturbations. Our results highlight the strengths and limitations of each approach, providing insight into the effectiveness of CP to complement common ensemble-based uncertainty quantification in simplified atmospheric models.

</details>


### [66] [A Generalization Theory for JEPA-Based World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27014)
*Jingyi Cui,Qi Zhang,Hongwei Wen,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为基于JEPA的世界模型建立了泛化理论，将其预训练表述为条件谱图学习问题，证明JEPA目标等价于动作条件共现矩阵的低秩分解，并建立了预训练误差与下游规划遗憾之间的联系，揭示了潜在维度在近似误差与样本误差之间的固有权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA作为世界建模的新范式在经验上取得了成功，但其理论基础仍然有限，缺乏对JEPA世界模型泛化性能的理论理解。

Method: 将JEPA预训练形式化为条件谱图学习问题；证明JEPA目标等价于动作条件共现矩阵的低秩分解；建立预训练误差与下游规划遗憾之间的理论联系；推导有限样本泛化界。

Result: 揭示了潜在维度在近似误差与样本误差之间的固有权衡；为理解隐式预测模型相对于输入级预测模型的优势与局限性提供了理论见解。

Conclusion: JEPA的泛化性能受潜在维度选择的权衡影响，理论分析为JEPA世界模型的设计和优化提供了指导。

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for world modeling by learning predictive dynamics in a latent space rather than generating future observations at the input level. Despite their empirical success, the theoretical understanding of JEPA-based world models remains limited. In this paper, we develop the first generalization theory for JEPA-based world models. We formulate JEPA pretraining as a conditional spectral graph learning problem and show that the JEPA objective is equivalent to a low-rank factorization of an action-conditioned co-occurrence matrix. Building on this characterization, we establish a connection between JEPA pretraining error and downstream planning regret, leading to a finite-sample generalization bound for JEPA-based world models. Our analysis reveals an inherent trade-off between approximation and sample errors with respect to the latent dimension, providing theoretical insights into the advantages and limitations of latent predictive models compared with input-level predictive approaches.

</details>


### [67] [Just how sure are you? Improving Verbalized Uncertainty Calibration in Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27023)
*Eren Senoglu,Federico Toschi,Nicolo Brunello,Andrea Sassella,Mark James Carman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型在医学视觉问答中过度自信的问题，本文提出一种训练框架，通过复合损失函数（包含Brier校准项、锚点正则化、对比图文对齐、KL模型稳定项）微调模型，在三个医学VQA基准和两种架构上将校准误差降低60%以上，判别力提升26%以上，同时保持预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在医学视觉问答中会产生过度自信的输出，而现有的口头置信度校准方法仅针对纯文本LLM开发，未考虑医学图像理解的多模态特性。

Method: 提出基于训练的微调框架，使用复合损失函数：(1) Brier风格校准项；(2) 锚点正则化防止置信度向极端值坍缩；(3) 对比图文对齐项；(4) KL模型稳定项。利用2×2因子扰动设计（交叉图像存在性与文本完整性）推导对齐信号，并通过top-K KL散度正则化保护模型回答能力。

Result: 在MedGemma 4B IT和Qwen2 VL 7B Instruct两种架构上，在三个医学VQA基准中校准误差降低60%以上，判别力提升26%以上，预测准确性保持。在平均性能上优于基于提示、采样和训练的方法。消融实验证实损失函数各组件均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 所提训练框架能有效提升多模态大语言模型在医学VQA任务中的置信度校准性能，各损失组件具有必要性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) applied to Medical Visual Question Answering (VQA) tend to produce overconfident outputs regardless of actual correctness, and existing verbalized confidence calibration methods, developed primarily for text only LLMs, do not account for the multimodal nature of medical image understanding.
  This work proposes a training based framework that finetunes MLLMs to improve their calibration using a composite loss function combining a Brier style calibration term, an anchor regularizer that prevents confidence collapse toward extreme values, a contrastive image text alignment term, and a KL based model stabilization term. The alignment signal is derived from a $2 \times 2$ factorial perturbation design that crosses image presence with text integrity, probing the reliance of the model on visual modality input versus language priors. Finally, a top K KL divergence regularizer is used to protect the answering ability of the model during finetuning.
  Across three Medical VQA benchmarks and two architectures (MedGemma 4B IT and Qwen2 VL 7B Instruct), our method reduces calibration error by 60% or more, and improves discrimination by 26% or more, while preserving predictive accuracy. On average across benchmarks, the technique outperforms prompting based, sampling based, and training based approaches, and ablation experiments confirm that each component of the loss function is indeed necessary for improving the calibration. All code for the experiments is publicly available.

</details>


### [68] [Symplectic Neural Networks for learning Generalized Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27029)
*Harsh Choudhary,Vyacheslav Kungurtsev,Chandan Gupta,Melvin Leok,Georgios Korpas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用隐式辛积分器高效训练哈密顿神经网络（HNN）的方法，通过伴随系统的辛离散化实现反向传播，并采用预测-校正ODE求解器和不动点迭代降低计算成本，在不可分混沌系统的系统辨识和能量守恒方面展现出数值优势。此外，利用后向误差分析对学习到的哈密顿量进行后处理，无需更精确的流映射离散化即可获得更准确的近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从含噪声的状态变量观测中识别系统哈密顿量是一项具有挑战性的任务，而现有HNN在使用隐式辛积分器时面临计算成本高和通过ODE求解器反向传播困难的问题。

Method: 利用伴随系统的辛离散化与反向传播的等价性来训练HNN参数；采用预测-校正ODE求解器和不动点迭代缓解隐式时间步进的计算开销；使用后向误差分析对学习到的哈密顿量进行后处理。

Result: 在不可分混沌系统的系统辨识和能量守恒任务上展示了数值优势，同时实现了高效的计算和内存复杂度。

Conclusion: 后向误差分析得到的修正哈密顿量是真实哈密顿量的更准确近似，无需使用更精确的流映射离散化。

Abstract: Hamiltonian Neural Networks (HNNs) integrate physical priors into neural models by learning a system's Hamiltonian, improving generalization and sample efficiency. Identifying the system Hamiltonian from noisy observations of state variables is a challenging task. For simulations to faithfully reflect the long-term behavior of Hamiltonian systems, especially energy conservation, it is essential to use symplectic integrators, which preserve the system's geometric structure. This fidelity comes at a cost: implicit symplectic integrators are more computationally intensive and make backpropagation through the ODE solver non-trivial. However, by leveraging the fact that symplectic discretizations of the adjoint system yield the same sensitivities associated by backpropagation, we obtain an efficient method of training the Neural Network parameters. In our work, we explore this alternate method of HNN training under noisy observation of trajectories with our HNN model based on an implicit symplectic integrator. Computationally, a predictor-corrector based ODE solver and fixed point iteration help to mitigate the computational cost of the implicit timestepping, resulting in more efficient generation of gradient updates. We showcase the numerical advantage, in experiments, in system identification and energy preservation on a range of non-separable, chaotic systems and the efficient computation and memory complexity of our method. We also observe that the post-processing of the learned Hamiltonian using backward error analysis yields a modified Hamiltonian that is a more accurate approximation of the true Hamiltonian without the need to use more accurate discretizations of the flow map.

</details>


### [69] [State Representation Matters in Deep Reinforcement Learning: Application to Energy Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27032)
*Jesper Klicks,Sander Vržina,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了抽水蓄能交易环境中状态表示对强化学习智能体性能的影响。通过保持环境、动作空间、网络等固定，仅改变市场特征（绝对价格、相对特征、预测特征及其组合），发现单一特征族效果有限（绝对特征仅达28.8%），而组合特征（绝对+相对+预测）显著提升性能（测试集55.6%，跨区域中位数47.5%）。结论：状态表示是储能交易RL策略设计的核心，而非预处理环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源交易决策不仅依赖当前市场价格，还受未来市场条件和运行约束影响，因此强化学习中状态表示的设计至关重要。现有研究对状态特征选择缺乏系统性分析，本文旨在探究不同市场特征族及其组合对抽水蓄能套利交易策略性能的影响。

Method: 基于HydroDam抽水蓄能套利环境，使用固定的Double DQN智能体，保持环境、动作空间、奖励函数、网络结构和训练协议不变，仅改变市场特征。比较绝对价格/日历特征、相对特征（比较当前价格与近期历史）、预测特征三类特征族及其组合。使用2007-2011年比利时日前价格训练，在2012-2025年同一市场测试集和39个ENTSO-E市场区域上进行评估。

Result: 绝对特征仅达测试集28.8%、跨区域中位数5.7%；相对特征和预测特征单独使用时均低于滚动价格评分启发式方法的跨区域中位数。组合特征显著提升：绝对+相对达测试集49.9%、跨区域中位数39.8%；绝对+相对+预测达测试集55.6%、跨区域中位数47.5%。

Conclusion: 状态表示是储能交易强化学习策略设计的核心组成部分，而非次要的预处理选择。鲁棒的跨市场迁移需要结合价格尺度、近期相对价格上下文和短期预测信息，而非依赖任何单一特征族。

Abstract: Energy trading decisions depend not only on current market prices, but also on expected future market conditions, and operational constraints. This makes the state representation given to a reinforcement learning agent an important design choice. We study this in HydroDam, a pumped-storage arbitrage environment, using a fixed Double DQN agent. The environment, action space, reward function, network, and training protocol are kept fixed; only the market features are changed. We compare absolute price/calendar features, relative features that compare current prices with recent market history, forecast features, and all combinations of these three feature families. Policies are trained and selected using 2007--2011 Belgian day-ahead prices and evaluated on two test settings: a later same-market test set from 2012--2025 and 39 other ENTSO-E market zones. Absolute features only reaches 28.8% on the test set and a median 5.7% across zones. Relative-only and forecast-only states also stay below a rolling price-score heuristic in the cross-zone median. Combining feature families is much stronger: absolute + relative reaches 49.9% on the test set and a 39.8% cross-zone median, while absolute + relative + forecast reaches 55.6% and 47.5%. These results suggest that state representation is not a minor preprocessing choice in storage-trading RL, but a central part of the policy design: robust transfer requires combining price scale, recent relative price context, and short-horizon forecast information, rather than relying on any single feature family.

</details>


### [70] [Finding Stationary Points by Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27082)
*Helin Wang,Chenyi Zhang,Xiwen Tao,Yexin Zhang,Tongyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅通过比较预言机（给定两点，输出哪点函数值更大）来寻找非凸函数驻点的问题。针对二阶可微且梯度与Hessian矩阵Lipschitz连续的函数，提出一个经典算法，使用$\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$次查询即可找到ε-驻点；并进一步提出量子比较预言机模型下的首个量子算法，仅需$\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$次查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际场景中（如黑箱优化、实验设计等），我们只能获得函数值的比较结果而非具体数值，因此研究仅通过比较预言机进行非凸优化具有重要理论价值和实际意义。

Method: 经典算法通过一个子程序来估计归一化Hessian矩阵，该子程序使用$\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$次查询达到精度δ；量子算法则利用量子叠加态进行查询，实现了维度依赖从n²降至n的加速。

Result: 经典算法：$\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$次查询找到ε-驻点；量子算法：$\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$次查询找到ε-驻点。

Conclusion: 本文首次在比较预言机模型下给出了非凸函数驻点搜索的量子加速算法，将查询复杂度从经典的n²维度依赖降低到n。

Abstract: We study the problem of finding stationary points of non-convex functions when access to the objective is provided only through a comparison oracle that, given two points, outputs which has the larger function value. For a twice differentiable $f\colon\mathbb R^n\to\mathbb R$ with Lipschitz gradient and Hessian, we develop an algorithm that visits an $ε$-stationary point using $\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$ queries. Our approach uses a subroutine that estimates the normalized Hessian to accuracy $δ$ using $\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$ queries. We further study this problem with a quantum comparison oracle model where queries can be made in superpositions, and develop the first quantum algorithm that finds an $ε$-stationary point, which takes $\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$ queries.

</details>


### [71] [Data-Free Reservoir Features for Efficient Long-Horizon Cold-Start Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27095)
*Augustinas Jučas,Yangchen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CIRCLE是一种无需回放、外部预训练或大初始任务的冷启动类增量学习方法，使用固定的双向二维储备池特征和流式线性判别分析头，在极多任务分割下显著优于现有方法，且训练速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冷启动类增量学习（CS-EFCIL）方法存在计算张力：漂移补偿方法需重复训练主干网络且随任务增长成本上升，而冻结主干方法虽廉价但在冷启动下性能较弱。作者探索第三种方案——完全不拟合图像数据的固定特征提取器，以平衡性能与效率。

Method: 提出CIRCLE方法，包含：(1) 固定的双向二维储备池特征提取器（BiRC2D），完全不经过图像数据训练；(2) 流式线性判别分析（SLDA）头，支持闭式流式更新；(3) 多随机储备池实例化组成特征集成，平均独立SLDA头的softmax输出，实现可调节的偏差-方差权衡。无需回放、任务边界信息或主干反向传播，支持样本级训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet、ImageNet-Subset和ImageNet-1k上，10-20任务分割时性能具竞争力；在50、100和500任务分割下显著优于强CS-EFCIL基线。消融实验表明BiRC2D风格提取器、SLDA头和平衡的特征/预测集成均贡献于最终性能。训练速度远快于基于训练主干的漂移补偿方法。

Conclusion: 固定随机特征提取器结合流式分类头是冷启动类增量学习的一种高效且有效的替代方案，在极长任务序列场景下优势尤为明显，为CS-EFCIL提供了新的研究方向和计算效率与性能之间的更好平衡。

Abstract: Cold-start exemplar-free class-incremental learning requires learning a growing set of classes without replay, external pretraining, or a large initial task. Existing cold-start methods typically either train the backbone throughout the stream and compensate for semantic drift, or freeze a backbone after the first task, producing features biased toward the initial classes. These choices also create a computational tension: drift-compensation methods require repeated backbone training and increasingly expensive updates as the task horizon grows, while frozen-backbone methods are cheap but weak under cold start. We study a third option: a feature extractor that is never fit to image data at all. We propose CIRCLE, a class-incremental classifier built from fixed bidirectional two-dimensional reservoir features, adapted from BiRC2D for image classification, and streaming linear discriminant analysis heads. CIRCLE groups multiple random reservoir instantiations into feature ensembles and averages the softmax outputs of independent SLDA heads, yielding a tunable bias-variance tradeoff between richer random features and prediction-level ensembling. Because the feature extractor is fixed and the head admits streaming closed-form updates, CIRCLE performs sample-wise training without replay, task-boundary information, or backbone backpropagation. On CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, ImageNet-Subset, and ImageNet-1k, CIRCLE is competitive at 10-20 task splits and substantially outperforms strong CS-EFCIL baselines at 50, 100, and 500 task splits, while training much faster than trained-backbone drift-compensation methods. Ablations show that the BiRC2D-style extractor, SLDA head, and balanced feature/prediction ensembling each contribute to the final performance.

</details>


### [72] [Transformer-Based Classification of Bacterial Raman Spectra with LOOCV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27096)
*Jamile Mohammad Jafari,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了基于Transformer的拉曼光谱分类方法，采用嵌套留一重复交叉验证框架，与PCA/ICA结合LDA、SVM和随机森林的传统机器学习管线进行对比。在包含5,417条单细胞光谱（6种细菌、9个独立测量重复）的数据集上，Transformer在独立测试重复中一致达到最高分类性能，显著优于传统方法；其学习的潜在特征空间实现了比PCA/ICA更好的类别分离，且可直接应用于未预处理的原始拉曼光谱，展现出跨测量重复的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拉曼光谱分类方法依赖PCA或ICA等线性降维技术，在处理高维、非线性光谱数据时存在局限性，且现有研究缺乏对独立测量重复间泛化性能的系统评估。作者旨在探索Transformer模型能否克服这些局限，并强调重复感知验证框架对模型评估的重要性。

Method: 采用嵌套留一重复交叉验证框架（nested leave-one-replicate-out cross-validation），将Transformer模型与PCA/ICA+LDA、SVM、随机森林等传统管线进行系统比较。数据集包含5,417条单细胞拉曼光谱，覆盖6种细菌物种和9个独立测量重复。

Result: Transformer在所有独立测试重复上一致取得最高分类性能，显著优于所有传统方法。其学习的潜在特征空间相比PCA/ICA表示具有更好的类别分离性。Transformer可直接应用于原始未预处理光谱仍保持优越性能，且在不同测量重复间表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: Transformer模型在拉曼光谱分类任务上具有显著潜力，其鲁棒性和优越性能强调了重复感知验证对于现实模型评估的重要性，为拉曼光谱分析提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently attracted increasing attention for Raman spectral classification. In this study, a transformer-based approach was systematically evaluated using a nested leave-one-replicate-out cross-validation framework and compared with conventional machine-learning pipelines combining PCA or ICA with LDA, SVM, and Random Forest classifiers. A bacterial Raman dataset comprising 5,417 single-cell spectra from six bacterial species and nine independent measurement replicates was used. The transformer consistently achieved the highest classification performance across independent test replicates and significantly outperformed all conventional approaches. Analysis of the learned latent feature space revealed improved class separation compared with PCA- and ICA-based representations. Furthermore, the transformer maintained superior performance when applied directly to raw Raman spectra without preprocessing, demonstrating robust behavior across measurement replicates. These findings highlight the potential of transformer-based models for robust Raman spectral classification and emphasize the importance of replicate-aware validation for realistic model evaluation.

</details>


### [73] [Heavy-Ball Q-Learning with Residual Weighting Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27112)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种修正的重球动量Q学习方法，建立了其收敛性，并证明了在特定条件下该方法比标准Q-learning收敛更快。该方法扩展至线性函数逼近的Q-learning，同样获得收敛性和加速保证。分析基于切换线性系统表示和联合谱半径，提供了理解重球动量加速Q-learning的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning方法收敛速度有限，而重球动量法在优化领域已被证明能加速收敛，但其在Q-learning中的应用缺乏严格的理论收敛性分析和加速条件刻画。

Method: 提出修正的重球动量Q-learning算法；将Q-learning算法建模为切换线性系统（SLS），并利用联合谱半径（JSR）分析收敛性和加速条件；将分析框架扩展至线性函数逼近场景。

Result: 建立了修正重球动量Q-learning的收敛性理论；给出了该方法比标准Q-learning收敛更快的充分条件；在线性函数逼近情形下同样获得了收敛性和加速性保证。

Conclusion: 切换线性系统和联合谱半径的视角为分析Q-learning算法提供了互补框架，并揭示了重球动量能够加速Q-learning的根本机制。

Abstract: This paper proposes a corrected heavy-ball Q-learning method for reinforcement learning (RL) and establishes its convergence. It also identifies conditions under which the method is theoretically guaranteed to converge faster than standard Q-learning. The same construction is then extended to Q-learning with linear function approximation, where analogous convergence and acceleration statements are derived. The analysis is based on a switched linear system (SLS) representation of Q-learning algorithms and on the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the associated switching families. This SLS viewpoint is not commonly used in standard analyses of Q-learning, and it provides a complementary framework and new insight into how heavy-ball momentum can accelerate Q-learning.

</details>


### [74] [Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network with Inverse Propensity Score under Unobserved Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27114)
*Haoran Zhang,Chuanpu Li,Yuxin Fu,Bin Tong,Guan Wang,Bo Zheng,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAUN和RA-IPS方法，分别通过跨头注意力机制和鲁棒对抗逆概率加权解决因果推断中的组间相似性建模与未观测混杂去偏问题，在三个数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升模型(Uplift modeling)面临两个核心挑战：一是需要灵活利用干预组与对照组之间的相似性来增强个体处理效应(ITE)的判别能力；二是在存在未观测混杂因素的情况下进行去偏估计。
