Method: 提出CHAUN（跨头注意力提升网络），使用共享特征嵌入和跨头注意力机制动态整合干预组与对照组的表征，增强组间相关性建模。理论证明在已知真实倾向分数时ITE可识别；对于未知真实倾向分数的场景，提出RA-IPS（鲁棒对抗逆概率加权），在约束不确定集内对抗优化倾向权重以缓解未观测变量带来的偏差。

Result: 在CRITEO-UPLIFT、LAZADA和工业电商数据集上，CHAUN相较于SOTA模型在QINI指标上最高提升25.6%；RA-IPS在未观测混杂场景下较标准IPS提升5.4%。

Conclusion: 提出的两种方法在真实因果推断任务中有效，验证了跨头注意力机制和鲁棒对抗优化在提升模型中的实用性。

Abstract: Uplift modeling, crucial for estimating individual treatment effects (ITE), faces dual challenges: flexibly leveraging inter-group similarity to enhance discriminative power and debiasing under unobserved confounding scenarios. In this paper, we propose the Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network (CHAUN) and Robust Adversarial Inverse Propensity Score (RA-IPS) method to address these limitations. CHAUN employs shared feature embeddings and cross-head attention mechanisms to dynamically integrate treatment-specific and control-specific representations, enhancing inter-group correlation modeling. Theoretically, we prove that access to the true propensity scores ensures ITE identifiability even with unobserved confounders. For practical scenarios lacking true propensity scores, RA-IPS adversarially optimizes propensity weights within constrained uncertainty sets to mitigate bias from unobserved variables. Experiments on public datasets (CRITEO-UPLIFT, LAZADA) and a production e-commerce dataset demonstrate CHAUN's superiority over state-of-the-art uplift models, achieving relative improvements of up to 25.6% in QINI scores. RA-IPS further enhances robustness, outperforming standard IPS by 5.4% under unobserved confounding. The results validate the effectiveness of our proposed methods in real-world causal inference tasks.

</details>


### [75] [Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) for aerodynamic prediction: a comparison with MLPs and GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27126)
*Miguel Jaraiz,Fermin Gutierrez,Pablo Yeste,Miguel Sánchez-Domínguez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了KAN在流体动力学代理建模（预测翼型表面压力分布）中的表现，发现KAN性能略逊于MLP，而GNN表现最佳但训练更耗时；KAN虽然复杂度低、训练快，但存在训练不稳定且高度依赖超参数优化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于KAN是否在传统任务中优于MLP存在争议，作者希望通过流体动力学代理建模这一具体领域，对比KAN、MLP和GNN的性能，以厘清KAN的实际优势与局限。

Method: 以亚音速和跨音速翼型表面压力分布预测为任务，在相同数据集上分别训练KAN、MLP和GNN模型，比较其预测性能、模型复杂度、训练速度及稳定性。

Result: KAN能较好预测压力系数并插值马赫数和攻角，但性能略逊于MLP；GNN性能最佳但训练耗时最长；KAN模型复杂度最低、训练最快，但训练不稳定且对超参数高度敏感。

Conclusion: 在流体动力学代理建模中，KAN并未展现出优于MLP或GNN的压倒性优势，其训练不稳定性和超参数敏感性是主要短板，KAN超越MLP的说法在该领域不成立。

Abstract: Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) have recently been introduced as a (deep) neural network architecture whose trainable parameters adapt the activation functions, instead of the coefficients of the affine transformations at the core of traditional architectures such as deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). This architecture builds on the Kolmogorov-Arnold theorem, which endows it with universal approximation properties. While the advent of KANs has been received with excitement, there is a current debate about the possible KAN supremacy over deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) for classic fields such as symbolic regression, generic-purpose machine learning, natural language processing or computer vision. Here we assess the performance of KANs --and its nuanced comparison against MLPs and graph neural networks (GNNs)-- in the realm of fluid dynamics surrogate modelling. To that aim, we consider the task of predicting the surface pressure distribution over subsonic and transonic airfoils, a canonical task in aerodynamics. Our results show that KAN models show good performance in predicting the whole pressure coefficients and is able to interpolate across Mach numbers and angles of attack, however its performance is comparable --marginally inferior-- to a suitably trained MLP, where best performance is achieved by a GNN at the expense or requiring lengthier training. While the optimal KAN model have typically much lower complexity than MLP and GNN --hence resulting in faster training--, we find that KANs suffer from training instabilities, and their performance is highly dependent on a proper hyperparameter optimisation.

</details>


### [76] [fTNN: a tensor neural network for fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27140)
*Qingkui Ma,Hehu Xie,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了fTNN，一种用于有界域上分数阶拉普拉斯问题（包括分数阶泊松方程和时间依赖的分数阶对流扩散方程）的确定性张量神经网络子空间方法。该方法采用几何自适应积分分裂，将分数阶拉普拉斯算子分解为奇异近场、规则中间远场和分析外部远场三个部分，通过高斯-雅可比求积、高斯求积和确定性的角度求积实现完全确定性的积分框架。为精确解析低正则性解和相关的损失泛函，构建了富含显式边界特征的边界奇异性感知试验函数，并提出了两种自动选择主导指数和评估损失函数的策略。对于时间依赖的分数阶PDE，设计了时空可分离神经网络，将时空残差分解为低维时间积分和空间积分之和，并集成了交替神经网络子空间优化策略。数值实验表明，所提框架在测试基准上达到了高精度，特别是在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上，显著优于现有的fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有界域上分数阶拉普拉斯问题的数值求解挑战，特别是传统分数阶PINN和蒙特卡洛方法在处理边界奇异性和长时间模拟时精度不足的问题。

Method: 1）几何自适应积分分裂将分数阶拉普拉斯分解为三部分；2）高斯-雅可比求积处理奇异径向积分，高斯求积处理规则径向积分，确定性角度求积处理角度变量；3）边界奇异性感知试验函数；4）自动选择主导指数与损失函数评估策略；5）时空可分离神经网络及交替子空间优化。

Result: 在测试基准上达到高精度，尤其在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上，显著优于现有fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的fTNN框架为分数阶拉普拉斯问题提供了一种高效、高精度的确定性求解方案，特别适用于具有边界奇异性特征和长时间演化的问题。

Abstract: We develop the fTNN, a deterministic tensor neural network subspace method for problems involving the fractional Laplacian on bounded domains, taking the fractional Poisson equation and time-dependent fractional advection-diffusion equation as typical representatives. The work employs a geometry-adapted integration split featuring a spatially dependent near-field radius, which decomposes the fractional Laplacian into three contributions: a singular near field, a regular interior far field, and an analytical exterior far field. Then the singular radial integrals are treated by Gauss-Jacobi quadrature, the regular radial integrals by Gauss quadrature, and the angular variables by deterministic angular quadrature, yielding a fully deterministic integration framework of the fractional Laplacian operator. To accurately resolve low-regularity solutions and the associated loss functional, we construct boundary-singularity-aware trial functions enriched with explicit boundary features, and propose two strategies for automatically selecting the leading exponent and evaluating the loss function from the singularity structure induced by the fractional operator, or jointly by the fractional operator and the source term. For time-dependent fractional PDEs, we design a spatiotemporally separable neural network that factorizes the time-space residual into a sum of low-dimensional temporal and spatial integrals, and we integrate this representation with an alternating neural network subspace optimization strategy for efficient training. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework attains high accuracy on the tested benchmarks and improves substantially over existing fPINN and Monte Carlo baselines, particularly for problems with strong boundary singularities and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [77] [Stochastic Gradient Optimization with Model-Assisted Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27171)
*Jonne Pohjankukka,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模型辅助采样框架，将小批量梯度估计视为调查抽样问题，利用辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器，在71-86%的实验中获得性能提升，尤其在使用动量优化器时能在约一半训练轮次内达到更好的泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的小批量随机梯度下降引入梯度噪声，在收敛稳定性、速度和泛化之间存在权衡。现有的方差缩减技术和自适应优化器虽能缓解梯度噪声，但可能引入额外计算开销。作者旨在弥合机器学习优化与调查抽样理论之间的鸿沟，从抽样理论视角重新审视梯度估计问题。

Method: 提出模型辅助采样框架，将数据集视为固定有限总体，梯度视为基于样本的估计量，借助辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器。均匀采样作为无辅助信息时的特例。该框架可轻松集成到现有优化器中，不改变其动力学特性。

Result: 在合成数据及六个基准数据集上的实验表明，在71-86%的实验中取得性能提升，尤其对中等规模输入空间效果显著。结合AdamW等动量优化器时，所提估计器在大约一半的训练轮次内即可实现更优的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过将调查抽样理论引入深度学习优化，模型辅助采样框架能有效降低梯度估计方差，提升优化效率，且与现有优化器兼容，是一种有前景的梯度估计改进方向。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

</details>


### [78] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecallRisk-BERT，一种结合PubMedBERT文本表示与结构化分类特征的多任务学习框架，用于FDA医疗设备召回记录自动分类（严重程度与根原因9分类）。基于54,165条真实数据，单任务LightGBM达0.963准确率，多任务RecallRisk-BERT显著优于基线，风险排序与真实模式高度一致（rho=0.983）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备召回记录数量不断增长，现有研究分别处理召回预测或根因分析，缺乏同时建模召回严重等级与根因类别的联合框架，影响自动分类与监管决策效率。

Method: 使用openFDA中54,165条召回记录（2002-2025），首先评估经典机器学习和Boosting模型（如LightGBM）；再提出RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，将PubMedBERT文本表示与结构化特征（产品代码、法规编号、医学专业）的嵌入表示融合，同时预测严重等级（3类）和根因类别（9类）。

Result: 单任务严重度预测中，LightGBM文本-表格融合配置最优：准确率0.963，宏F1 0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974。多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线，风险排序与真实根因严重模式高度一致（rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格联合学习可支持可扩展的召回后自动分类、监管决策辅助与基于模型的根因风险分析。

Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [79] [Automating Potential-based Reward Shaping with Vision Language Model Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27180)
*Henrik Müller,Daniel Kudenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-PBRS框架，利用轻量级视觉语言模型（VLM）的偏好反馈学习势函数，实现基于势能的奖励塑造（PBRS），在保证最优策略不变的前提下提升强化学习的样本效率并防止奖励破解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励场景下，智能体缺乏中间反馈引导探索，且易将稀疏的成功奖励错误归因；传统奖励塑造可能引发奖励破解；PBRS虽能保证最优策略集不变，但需要人工设计启发式势函数，成本高且难以泛化。

Method: 利用轻量级VLM对图像对进行偏好比较，基于这些偏好标签训练一个势函数模型；将该势函数用于PBRS框架中来塑造奖励信号；采用计算效率更高的小型VLM替代大型VLM以降低推理成本。

Result: 在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境下，VLM-PBRS在保持最优策略不变的同时，显著提升了样本效率，并展现出对奖励破解的鲁棒性。实验还揭示了VLM偏好标签准确率与样本效率提升之间的关联。

Conclusion: VLM-PBRS首次将VLM偏好学习应用于势函数合成，提供了一种低成本的PBRS解决方案，在稀疏奖励任务中有效平衡了理论保证与实用性能。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are inherently challenging for reinforcement learning agents as they lack intermediate feedback to guide exploration and to correctly attribute the sparse success rewards to relevant parts of the trajectory. Naive reward shaping can induce reward hacking, yielding policies that exploit auxiliary signals instead of solving the intended task. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) guarantees preservation of the optimal policy set, but requires the definition of a heuristic potential function over the state space. In this work, we introduce the VLM-guided PBRS framework VLM-PBRS that learns the potential function directly from vision language model (VLM) feedback. We query a lightweight VLM to obtain preferences over image pairs and train a model of the potential function using these preferences. As this approach is based on potential-based reward shaping, it preserves the original optimal policies, and removes the need for expert-designed reward shaping terms. Because large VLMs are prohibitively expensive to invoke repeatedly during policy learning, we employ smaller, more computationally efficient VLMs. Although the resulting preference labels are less accurate, empirical evidence shows that the preference labels can still be used to accelerate learning. We validate our method empirically in the Meta-World and Franka Kitchen environments and highlight the connection between VLM preference label accuracy and sample efficiency improvements. Our contributions are threefold: (1) the first application of VLM preference-based learning to synthesize a potential function for PBRS, (2) a principled, low-cost solution that leverages small VLMs, and (3) extensive empirical demonstration of improved sample efficiency and robustness to reward hacking.

</details>


### [80] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化相关测度(NRM)框架的完整信息流归因方法，用于解释事件型时序图神经网络(ETGNNs)，该方法通过模块化分解技术覆盖了所有事件相关变量的信息路径，在流行病追踪、社会动态和政治事件网络数据集上优于现有解释方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETGNN解释方法仅追踪事件相关嵌入到输出的部分信息流，忽略了通过事件诱导变量（mediating interactions between nodes）的重要路径，而这些路径对捕获长时序依赖至关重要。

Method: 基于NRM框架，提出完整信息流归因方法：(1)显式量化来自事件嵌入和通过事件诱导变量的信息流；(2)确保跨层潜在变量的可比性；(3)支持事件间交互的高阶分析；(4)引入模块化分解程序处理ETGNN架构复杂性，系统构建复杂神经架构的相关性结构。

Result: 在两个合成数据集（流行病追踪、社会动态）和一个真实世界政治事件网络数据集上的定性和定量实验表明，该方法一致优于现有解释方法，并产生更可解释的解释。

Conclusion: 通过覆盖完整信息流（包括事件诱导变量路径），该方法能更全面地解释ETGNN，为复杂时序图神经网络的归因解释提供了新范式。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [81] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了图神经网络（GNN）的设计空间、谱域与空间域的统一原理、Weisfeiler-Leman层次表达能力，并在12个应用领域中评估了图结构的成本效益，揭示了异质性、规模、时间动态等跨领域共性问题，以及学术排行榜架构与工业部署之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于回答一个关键问题：图结构在哪些场景下值得其计算开销，在哪些场景下不必要。随着GNN从 niche 技术成为默认模型，需要系统性地审视其实际价值而非默认使用。

Method: 采用统一设计空间方法，从共享第一原理推导谱域和空域公式，将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层级关联，并对12个应用领域逐一分析图构建选择与成本、主导架构家族及原因、区分真实收益与弱基线或有利数据划分带来的伪增益，最后进行跨领域比较。

Result: 跨领域比较发现：异质性和规模在几乎所有领域削弱相同模型；时序图普遍比静态图更难；公开排行榜上的顶尖架构很少被部署到实际系统中。此外，过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性成为决定模型能否被采纳的关键约束。

Conclusion: 图神经网络的价值高度依赖具体领域和任务，不能默认使用。需要更严谨的基线对比和成本效益分析，学术界与工业界在架构选择上存在显著差距，未来的研究应关注跨领域共性问题而非仅在排行榜上追逐性能。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

</details>


### [82] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FisherSketch，一种无需训练的源模型选择方法，通过估计Fisher对齐的余弦相似度（在联合激活-误差空间中），解决了共享词汇表的LLM家族（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列）中表示度量（如CKA）不可识别的问题，仅需单次流式扫描即可生成16KB任务签名和192KB流式状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学字符串领域（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列）中，候选语料库共享分词器但预测目标不同，导致"激活-暗"状态：表示相似性度量在缺乏标签条件误差几何假设时失效，而经典更新几何度量在词汇表规模下计算不可行。

Method: 理论证明在共享输出头设置下，表示度量（如CKA）不可识别；关键恒等式表明头Fisher对齐恰好是联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦，分解为激活、误差和耦合因子。FisherSketch通过单次流式扫描直接估计该余弦，K=128,256的头Fisher对齐只需16KB任务签名和192KB流式状态。

Result: 在共享参数和内部层验证中有效，在Llama-3.1-8B词汇器偏移实验中，当激活相似性无法区分任务时，FisherSketch仍能保持信息量。

Conclusion: FisherSketch为共享词汇表的LLM家族提供了一种计算高效、且能编码迁移相关更新结构的源选择方法，同时其签名和边际量可作为诊断工具研究LLM任务相似性的驱动因素（激活、误差或耦合）。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [83] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维生成对抗网络（GAN）的可解模型，将线性生成器的学习问题扩展至具有结构化潜在协方差的数据。通过引入类依赖、相关且非零均值的潜在结构，证明了微观训练过程在高维极限下收敛到确定性常微分方程，并揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关可提升弱方向的可学习性，而过强相关会破坏恢复稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的可解GAN分析仅假设无条件信号和对角潜在协方差，未考虑实际数据中常见的类依赖、相关性和非零均值结构。本文旨在扩展理论框架，揭示这些异质性特征如何影响GAN的训练动态和学习能力。

Method: 采用高维统计力学方法，构建具有二次能量判别器的线性生成器GAN模型，引入类依赖、相关且非零均值的潜在协方差结构。通过证明随机微观训练过程在高维极限下收敛到确定性常微分方程，利用有效二阶矩刻画异质性的影响，并进行稳定性分析与数值验证。

Result: 证明了所有异质性通过概率加权的有效二阶矩进入动力学。在匹配协方差特例下，稳定性分析给出了由学习率和噪声水平决定的模态可解区间：当主导有效特征值超过下阈值时学习开始，所有相关有效模态保持在区间内才能完全恢复。揭示了低秩相关可提升弱方向可学习性，过强相关则破坏稳定性的信号增强机制。数值模拟、MNIST、FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文拓展了可解GAN理论至更真实的数据结构，揭示了潜在协方差结构对GAN学习动态的关键影响，特别是信号增强与稳定性权衡机制，为设计生成器潜在空间提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [84] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RSPC语料库（1,799条Reddit帖子，由精神科医生标注），用于在人际关系情境中建模心理健康状态。通过基准测试发现Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类上最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上最强（Macro-F1=0.519），焦虑障碍与关系不确定性显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康研究将精神疾病建模为孤立现象，忽略了人际情境和关系触发因素。作者希望通过引入关系情境，从个体中心转向情境感知的心理健康建模。

Method: 构建RSPC语料库：从Reddit长距离关系板块收集1,799条帖子，由精神科医生标注诊断类别（焦虑、抑郁等）、关系压力触发因素和关系阶段。基准测试7个微调Transformer模型和5个LLM，涵盖多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务。

Result: Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类上Macro-F1=0.538最佳；GPT-4o在关系触发检测上Macro-F1=0.519最强。存在明显的任务依赖模型差异。焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性显著相关。

Conclusion: RSPC建立了考虑关系情境的NLP任务基准，支持从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变，捕捉痛苦的社会和时间动态。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [85] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文创建了一个来自Instagram和Reddit的赌博广告数据集，并对其进行了操纵性和欺骗性广告实践的手动标注，包含分类标签和人类推理解释，旨在实现可解释的操纵性广告检测，并分析了这些广告策略对用户心理健康的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上赌博应用广告日益增多，这些广告使用可能误导用户、鼓励风险行为并影响心理健康的说服技巧，但缺乏公开可用的标注数据集来研究自动检测操纵性和欺骗性赌博广告的方法。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个社交媒体平台收集赌博相关广告，进行手动标注操纵性和欺骗性广告实践，不仅提供分类标签，还包含人工解释说明标注推理过程，支持可解释性方法研究。同时分析赌博广告中常用的策略及其对用户心理健康的影响。

Result: 构建了一个包含人工标注和解释的赌博广告数据集，识别了常见的操纵性广告策略，建立了可支持可解释检测方法的框架。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于开发浏览器插件向用户预警操纵性赌博广告，以及帮助监管机构自动监控和检测在线赌博推广的自动网络爬虫等实际应用。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [86] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了时间序列预测中"更大模型带来更高精度"的主流观点，提出通过优化预处理（上下文长度、归一化、正则化、数据增强）而非扩大模型规模即可大幅缩小性能差距。使用Ridge回归作为实验平台，在8个基准上发现三个关键模式：最优回溯长度具有强序列特异性且与预测 horizon 呈非单调关系、在上下文末尾部分而非整体进行归一化更优、不同序列间超参数共享程度各异。优化后的线性模型在多数场景下超越Transformer/MLP/CNN等复杂模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究倾向于使用越来越大的架构（从专用Transformer到通用基础模型），假设模型容量是提升精度的关键。本文持相反立场，试图证明通过精心调整预处理策略，即便使用简单的线性模型也能达到甚至超越大型模型的性能，从而揭示数据预处理的重要性被学界严重低估。

Method: 以Ridge回归（具有闭式解和可解释权重）为测试平台，系统搜索上下文长度、局部归一化（学习最优归一化窗口比例）、正则化强度和时序数据增强四个预处理维度。在8个标准基准（ETTm2、Exchange、Traffic等）上开展网格搜索，分析超参数与数据特征之间的模式关系。

Result: (1) 最优回溯长度与序列强相关，且与预测步长呈非单调关系，幂律指数从+0.46到-0.19不等，推翻"长预测需长历史"的惯例。(2) 在上下文末尾部分而非整体进行归一化几乎普遍更优。(3) 同一数据集内不同序列的最佳超参数存在分歧，跨序列共享程度需因数据集而异。优化后的线性模型在6/8个基准上超过Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。

Conclusion: 预处理优化可以低成本弥合与大型架构的性能差距；优化后的超参数可作为数据诊断工具，揭示大型模型通过可学习参数隐含吸收的数据结构信息。这提示研究社区应更多关注数据层面的设计而非单纯扩大模型规模。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [87] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对从多组解观测数据中学习控制方程的基本问题，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了从线性到非线性( Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场) ODE的可辨识性边界，并基于度量熵估计分析了样本复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学机器学习方法虽能从观测数据中学习控制方程，但缺乏严格的理论条件说明何时能从多个解观测中唯一且稳定地辨识出真实ODE，文献中也缺乏此类学习任务样本复杂度的定量分析。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的度量，该距离通过所有允许初值条件下两方程的最坏情况分离度来刻画辨识问题的极小极大结构；在此基础上推导可辨识性边界，并利用度量熵估计分析样本复杂度。

Result: 建立了从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场的非线性类别等广泛结构方程的可辨识性边界，量化了两个不同方程可从解数据中区分的精确条件；给出了样本复杂度界，说明需要多少个解观测才能可靠恢复控制方程。

Conclusion: 本文为从解观测数据中学习控制方程提供了理论可辨识性基础，填补了该领域样本复杂度理论分析的空白。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [88] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套端到端的RLAIF框架用于生成可迁移的求职搜索查询词，并系统研究了不同优化器在对抗性奖励面下的表现。研究表明，无评论家优化器的性能主要取决于奖励塑造，而非算法选择；GRPO因其组相对优势归一化机制对虚假奖励信号尤为敏感；引入基于规则的经验奖励下限可有效缓解奖励破解问题，但训练时的奖励模型会将性能增益膨胀2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职搜索平台的低频查询接口难以捕捉候选人档案的高维复杂性，现有方法缺乏能够抽象掉求职者身份信息的同时保留可泛化资质特征的"可移植"查询词生成能力。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架，采用LLM作为裁判提供奖励信号。系统比较了多种策略优化器（RLOO、REINFORCE++、GRPO），并引入确定性基于规则的奖励下限（reward floor）来修正逐字复制行为的奖励分配。

Result: 无评论家优化器的性能主要取决于奖励塑造而非算法选择；GRPO因组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号敏感；引入奖励下限后跨族评估裁判上质量提升+0.147；训练时奖励模型将性能增益膨胀2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功根本上依赖于奖励塑造纪律的执行，而非选择不同的优化器；GRPO在存在虚假奖励信号时尤为脆弱，需要额外的奖励修正机制。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [89] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真度迁移学习框架，结合轻量级物理仿真、卷积自编码器（CAE）深度特征学习、前馈神经网络和少量实验数据，实现了板状结构中损伤的准确定位与量化，R²分数分别超过0.93和0.99，为导波结构健康监测提供了高效实用的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导波结构健康监测（GWSHM）中深度学习模型的实际部署受到标记实验数据稀缺以及大规模高保真仿真数据集计算成本高昂的制约。

Method: 构建多保真度迁移学习框架：使用一维时域谱元模型生成大量合成数据用于预训练；采用卷积自编码器（CAE）进行深度特征学习；结合前馈神经网络；利用少量标记实验数据通过迁移学习进行领域自适应。

Result: CAE迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于CNN对应方法；损伤定位R²>0.93，损伤量化R²>0.99；在预训练或微调中未见过的损伤场景中仍保持高预测精度。

Conclusion: 该框架准确、计算高效且具有实际可行性，是面向真实GWSHM应用的可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [90] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度平衡（GEQ）与Blackwell可逼近性在算法上等价，进而通过已知等价关系，GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等在线学习框架也等价，并建立了高效的归约方法和精细保证的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEQ作为在线优化框架，与遗憾最小化等经典框架的关系尚不明确，已有工作指出GEQ误差与遗憾是不可比较的指标，因此需要精确定位GEQ在在线学习图景中的位置。

Method: 通过构造双向归约：一是将Blackwell可逼近问题归约为对黑箱GEQ预言机的查询（无渐近损失），二是反之亦然；利用已有可逼近性与遗憾最小化、校准之间的等价关系，推导GEQ与这些框架的等价性；同时分析了无约束和有约束决策集下不同GEQ概念之间的归约。

Result: 建立了GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性、遗憾最小化、校准之间的等价关系；提供了高效归约，可将乐观性、强自适应性等精细保证从遗憾最小化迁移到GEQ；识别了GEQ的必要充分条件。

Conclusion: GEQ并非独立于主流在线学习框架，而是与Blackwell可逼近性、遗憾最小化、校准在算法层面等价，从而统一了这些看似不同的范式。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与Top-k稀疏自编码器架构兼容的稀疏正则化方法（$\ell_1$惩罚和尺度不变的$\ell_1/\ell_2$比率惩罚），在不牺牲重建质量的前提下提升单义性，并发现硬性架构稀疏性与软性稀疏正则化具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k SAE虽然避免了传统$\ell_1$惩罚的缺陷，但存在固定预算k（无法适应输入复杂度变化）和过拟合训练k值的局限性。为此，作者希望在保留Top-k架构优势的同时引入软性稀疏正则化来弥补这些不足。

Method: 在Top-k选择之前，对预激活施加两种正则化：(1) 对未被Top-k选中的单元（off-support）施加$\ell_1$惩罚；(2) 尺度不变的$\ell_1/\ell_2$比率惩罚，使编码集中在更少的有效单元上。两种惩罚仅作用于batch内至少被选中一次的单元（batch-active units）。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型和多个k值下，两种正则化方法均一致地提升了单义性，且不损害重建质量。$\ell_1/\ell_2$惩罚进一步将信息集中在更少的隐单元上，使重建对推理时的k选择更鲁棒，并提升了小预算线性探针的性能。

Conclusion: 硬性架构稀疏性（Top-k）与软性稀疏正则化并非互斥，而是互补的，二者结合可以更有效地提升稀疏自编码器的性能。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [92] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，通过MMBench2数据集和覆盖率感知技术，实现了对幻觉的检测与缓解，仅需50条真实轨迹即可将预训练模型适应全新环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式世界模型虽能生成逼真的可控未来帧，但经常出现幻觉——视觉上流畅但偏离真实动态。作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，且轻量级数据中心信号既能检测幻觉也能指导缓解。

Method: 构建427小时、210任务的MMBench2数据集，训练350M参数世界模型；识别三种幻觉模式（感知型、动作边缘化型、场景发散型）；开发三种预测信号；提出覆盖率感知采样技术（训练时）和基于好奇心奖励的在线数据收集策略（微调时）。

Result: 所提出的幻觉预测信号能准确预测模型失败位置；覆盖率感知采样和好奇心驱动微调仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可将预训练世界模型适应全新环境，实现了数据高效的迁移。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的信号同样可用于缓解幻觉，揭示了数据中心视角下世界模型幻觉的统一解决框架。

Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

</details>


### [93] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出误差条件神经求解器(ENS)，将PDE残差场作为网络输入而非优化目标，使网络能学习迭代校正策略。在四个PDE族中，ENS在大多数设置下取得最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上提升达10倍，且避免了混合方法的高计算成本，在分布偏移下具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将求解视为纯统计任务，训练后无法纠正自身违反约束的问题且难以外推；混合方法通过梯度下降或高斯-牛顿步最小化PDE残差来提升物理正确性，但继承了底层经典优化器的计算成本和不稳定性。本文理论和实验表明，在病态系统中最小化PDE残差可能是重建精度不可靠的代理指标，这解释了为何混合方法尽管残差低却预测不准确。

Method: 提出误差条件神经求解器(ENS)：不同于将PDE残差作为优化目标，ENS将残差场作为网络每步迭代的直接输入，使网络能读取自身误差的空间结构，学习更新策略以迭代校正预测。

Result: 在四个PDE族上，ENS在大多数设置下达到最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流上提升达10倍，同时避免了混合方法的高计算成本。ENS的校正策略在分布偏移下具有泛化能力，包括零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移，在病态区域（残差最小化最不可靠的区域）相对优势最大。

Conclusion: 数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中可能成为重建精度的不可靠代理指标；将残差场作为输入而非优化目标，让网络学习迭代校正策略，能有效提升预测精度和泛化能力，同时保持计算效率。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [94] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），通过自回归建模框架替代传统的流模型，解决了玻尔兹曼生成器中基于归一化流模型在表达性和计算效率上的局限性。ArBG在多个基准测试中显著优于流模型，尤其在大型肽系统上表现突出，并提出了1.32亿参数的可迁移模型Robin，将零样本能量误差降低60%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻尔兹曼生成器主要依赖归一化流模型，但离散时间流存在严格可逆性约束导致表达性受限，连续时间流则面临计算昂贵的似然估计问题。本文旨在突破流模型的拓扑约束，实现更高效、可扩展的平衡态分子采样。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），采用自回归建模框架替代基于流的范式。该方法规避了流的拓扑约束，支持推断时的序列干预，并利用在大型语言模型中有效的架构实现更好的可扩展性。

Result: ArBG在所有基准测试中显著优于流模型，尤其在10残基的Chignolin等大型肽系统上提升明显。提出的132M参数可迁移模型Robin在8残基系统上的零样本能量误差E-W₂相比此前最优结果降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归玻尔兹曼生成器成功克服了流模型的根本性限制，为分子平衡态采样提供了更强大、可扩展的替代方案，并在零样本迁移中展现出优异的泛化能力。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [95] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励信号（无需真实答案）训练LLM进行分数优化任务，在提升启发式竞赛成绩的同时，也能迁移提升精确求解基准的表现，优于直接使用原始执行分数的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖真实答案分配奖励，限制了其在无真实解场景（如分数优化任务）中的应用。作者旨在探索如何在不依赖真实答案的情况下，利用连续的确定性执行反馈作为监督信号来训练LLM。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，包含两个关键组件应对连续奖励中的挑战：(1) 校准奖励塑造（calibrated reward shaping），通过实例级别比较来克服"尺度主导"问题；(2) 强调排名靠前的求解器，同时对其他有效解保留有界反馈，以克服"频率主导"问题。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，RiVER使Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414在ALE基准上分别提升8.9%和9.4%的排名；在LiveCodeBench和USACO上分别绝对平均提升2.4%和3.5%。基线方法虽能提升ALE评分但无法迁移到精确求解基准。

Conclusion: 分数优化任务结合恰当的奖励校准，可以在没有真实答案的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境，且训练效果能够迁移到精确求解任务上。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Hot AI in Cold Space: Thermal-Crosstalk-Aware Scheduling for Sustainable Orbital AI Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26150)
*Shuyi Chen,Zhengchang Hua,Nikos Tziritas,Georgios Theodoropoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出轨道数据中心（ODCs）在低延迟分布式大模型训练中面临"近距-热悖论"——极端物理密度导致热流体串扰和热辐射串扰，引发热停滞、性能下降和硬件寿命缩短。为解决这一问题，提出热感知异构性论点和热负载均衡（TLB）框架，通过动态迁移LLM负载到最冷的可用单元，缓解热瓶颈、恢复计算利用率并延长硬件寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面AI训练面临不可持续的能源和水资源危机，轨道数据中心虽被视为"零运营碳"方案，但分布式大模型训练所需的亚10微秒通信延迟迫使ODCs进入极端物理密度，引发严重的热管理问题，威胁轨道AI的可持续性。

Method: 提出热感知异构性论点，将空间冷却差异视为主要资源管理维度；在此基础上设计热负载均衡（TLB）软件框架，根据瞬时流体温度或吸收辐射动态将LLM工作负载迁移到最冷的可用单元。

Result: TLB能够消除热瓶颈，恢复模型算力利用率（MFU），同时降低物理热应力，延长轨道硬件使用寿命。

Conclusion: 延长轨道硬件寿命对于摊销火箭发射的巨大隐含碳至关重要，TLB为在不加剧电子垃圾的情况下规模化轨道AI提供了必要路径。

Abstract: Terrestrial AI training faces an unsustainable energy and water crisis, positioning Orbital Data Centers (ODCs) as a "zero operational carbon" alternative. However, the sub-$10μ\text{s}$ communication latency required for distributed Large Language Model (LLM) training forces ODCs into extreme physical density, triggering a critical "Proximity-Thermal Paradox." As these high-density systems scale into Monolithic Structures or Proximity Swarms, they suffer from intense thermal-fluid crosstalk (heat traps in shared cooling loops) and thermal-radiative crosstalk (mutual heating that blocks deep-space cooling radiators). If left unmitigated, this persistent heat stagnation not only triggers severe thermal throttling that degrades training throughput, but also induces severe thermal fatigue, drastically shortening hardware lifespans and generating premature space e-waste. To make orbital AI truly sustainable, this position paper challenges traditional uniform load-sharing. We propose the Thermal-Aware Heterogeneity Thesis, which treats spatial cooling variances as a primary resource management dimension. Building on this, we introduce Thermal-Load Balancing (TLB), a software framework that dynamically migrates LLM workloads to the coolest available units based on instantaneous fluid temperatures or absorbed radiation. Our analysis demonstrates that TLB resolves thermal bottlenecks to restore Model Flops Utilization (MFU), while simultaneously reducing physical thermal stress. Extending the operational lifespan of orbital hardware is crucial to amortize the massive embodied carbon of rocket launches, outlining a necessary pathway to scale orbital AI without accelerating e-waste.

</details>


### [97] [FinWhale: An Optimally Resilient Two-Round Terminating DAG Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26292)
*Razya Ladelsky,Roy Friedman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FinWhale 是首个基于 DAG 的拜占庭容错协议，在部分同步模型下实现了两消息延迟的快速提交路径，同时兼容原有慢速路径规则，支持 n=3f+2p-1 的验证器配置，容忍最多 f 个拜占庭故障，并在最多 p 个验证器出故障时仍能实现快速终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 DAG 的 BFT 协议至少需要三个消息延迟才能提交决策，而快速路径 BFT 协议虽可在有利条件下实现最优的两消息延迟终止，但无法自然地扩展到 DAG 结构。因此，需要一种将快速路径机制集成到 DAG 共识中的方案。

Method: FinWhale 扩展了 Mysticeti 协议，引入了一种基于快速路径证据块的新型提交结构，使快速路径提交机制能与原有的慢速路径规则安全共存。通过在本地 DAG 视图中使用证据块，验证器可以一致地结合快速路径和慢速路径的推理逻辑来保证安全性。

Result: FinWhale 在部分同步模型下，以 n=3f+2p-1 个验证器运行（匹配快速拜占庭共识的已知下界），容忍最多 f 个拜占庭故障，且在故障验证器数量不超过 p（1≤p≤f）时实现快速终止。结果表明，最优延迟快速路径可成功集成到非认证（uncertified）DAG 共识协议中。

Conclusion: 快速路径机制可以有效集成到基于 DAG 的 BFT 协议中，在不牺牲安全性的前提下实现最优的两消息延迟提交，为高性能 DAG 共识协议的设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols provide high throughput consensus under partial synchrony but existing DAG protocols still require at least three message delays to commit decisions. In contrast fast path Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols can achieve optimal two message delay termination under favorable conditions though they do not naturally extend to DAGs.
  We present FinWhale the first DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocol with a two message delay fast path. FinWhale extends Mysticeti with a novel fast path commit mechanism that safely coexists with the protocol's original slow path rules. To preserve safety across different local DAG views we introduce new commit structures based on fast path evidence blocks enabling validators to combine fast path and slow path reasoning consistently.
  FinWhale operates in the partially synchronous model with n equals three f plus two p minus one validators matching the known lower bound for fast Byzantine consensus. The protocol tolerates up to f Byzantine faults and achieves fast termination whenever at most p validators fail during the fast path where p is between one and f. Our results show that optimal latency fast paths can be integrated into uncertified DAG consensus protocols.

</details>


### [98] [A Distributed Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm Simulator for Engineering Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26297)
*Ali Rajabi,Milad Hasanzadeh,Amin Kargarian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个兼容Qiskit的分布式量子近似优化算法（DQAOA）模拟器，用于解决工程设计中的QUBO问题，支持单QPU的 monolithic QAOA 和多QPU的分布式QAOA，并提供了Streamlit GUI界面，通过参数化电路复用、批量评估等优化显著降低运行时开销，在标准基准测试和发电机组启停应用中验证了结果一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏一个可复用的模拟器，能够同时求解和比较不同QAOA执行模式（单QPU与多QPU分布式）下的QUBO实例，满足工程设计和决策应用中的实际需求。

Method: 提出DQAOA模拟器，工作流包括：规范化QUBO模型、映射为代价哈密顿量、在QPUs间分配变量、识别局部和跨QPU耦合、构建对应量子电路。采用参数化电路复用、固定深度目标复用、批量评估和并行多起点执行等运行时优化。提供Streamlit GUI支持无脚本交互。

Result: 在标准QUBO基准测试和发电机组启停（unit commitment）应用中，暴力搜索、monolithic QAOA和分布式QAOA均恢复相同的承诺比特串和运行成本。分阶段运行时分析显示实现阶段间运行时显著减少，但分布式QAOA因跨QPU耦合需要远程操作而计算成本更高。

Conclusion: 该模拟器能够产生与经典monolithic QAOA参考一致的结果（最优比特串和成本），验证了分布式QAOA在工程优化问题中的可行性和正确性，同时证明了所提出优化策略的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a Qiskit-compatible distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (DQAOA) simulator for quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problems arising in engineering design and decision applications. The open-source simulator is available through the RAISE LAB website and GitHub repository, with README documentation for installation, input formatting, configurable parameters, and example workflows. The package addresses the need for a reusable simulator that can solve and compare QUBO instances across different QAOA execution modes. It supports monolithic QAOA on a single quantum processing unit (QPU) and distributed QAOA across a user-specified number of QPUs with configurable capacities. The workflow canonicalizes the QUBO model, maps it to a cost Hamiltonian, allocates variables across QPUs, identifies local and cross-QPU couplings, and constructs the corresponding circuits. Runtime optimizations, including parameterized circuit reuse, objective reuse at fixed depth, batched evaluations, and parallel multi-start execution, reduce repeated overhead. A Streamlit graphical user interface is also provided for entering or uploading QUBO instances, configuring solver settings, running selected modes, and visualizing solution-quality metrics without editing Python scripts. The package is demonstrated on standalone QUBO benchmarks and a power generation unit commitment application. In the unit commitment case, brute force, monolithic QAOA, and distributed QAOA recover the same commitment bitstring and operating cost. Across multiple case studies, the simulator produces results consistent with classical monolithic QAOA references in terms of optimal bitstrings and costs. Staged runtime analysis shows substantial runtime reduction across implementation stages, while distributed QAOA remains more demanding because cross-QPU couplings require remote operations.

</details>


### [99] [Priceless: An examination of Serverless Functions-as-a-Service (FaaS) pricing models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26308)
*Nnamdi Ekwe-Ekwe*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对 AWS Lambda、Microsoft Azure Functions 和 Google Cloud Functions 三大 FaaS 提供商的功能特性及定价模型进行了详细比较分析，发现不同提供商之间以及同一提供商的不同区域之间存在显著的成本差异。AWS 总体运行成本最低，而 Azure 最昂贵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自无服务器函数即服务（FaaS）问世十年来，各提供商的功能不断扩展，但随之而来的是日益复杂且各不相同的定价模型。然而，缺乏对这些定价模型及其对实际工作负载成本的系统性比较研究，使用户难以在不同提供商之间做出经济高效的选择。

Method: 首先对 AWS Lambda、Azure Functions 和 Google Cloud Functions 的功能特性与定价模型进行详细梳理（称为 Priceless 框架）；然后选取示例工作负载，在三家平台上分别进行运行测试，开展跨提供商及跨区域的成本对比分析。

Result: 研究发现：（1）不同 FaaS 提供商之间运行相同工作负载的成本差异显著；（2）同一提供商内部，不同区域之间的成本也存在较大差异；（3）在三个主流提供商中，AWS Lambda 整体运行成本最低，而 Microsoft Azure Functions 对于等效工作负载最为昂贵。

Conclusion: FaaS 提供商的功能丰富化和定价模型差异化为用户带来了显著的成本不确定性。用户在选型时需综合考虑跨提供商和跨区域的价格差异，以优化无服务器应用的成本效益。AWS 在当前市场中具有明显的成本优势。

Abstract: Serverless Functions-as-a-Service providers have grown in their offering since inception a decade ago, with a myriad of new functionalities offered to end-users. These new features have also brought new, varied and at times complex pricing models that differ between providers. In this paper, we present Priceless, a detailed examination of the current state of the art of FaaS features, and their pricing models. We then perform a comparative price analysis of running example workloads across AWS Lambda, Microsoft Azure Functions and Google Cloud Functions. Our work finds significant cost differences both cross-provider, but also, cross-region within the provider. We find that AWS is the cheapest overall to run functions on, with Microsoft Azure being the most expensive for equivalent workloads.

</details>


### [100] [Moebius: Serving Mixture-of-Expert Models with Seamless Runtime Parallelism Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26607)
*Shaoyu Wang,Yizhuo Liang,Jaeyong Song,Chong Li,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Moebius 是一个 MoE 模型 serving 系统，支持在运行时动态切换张量并行（TP）和专家并行（EP）模式（无需重启或中断请求），通过利用两种并行方式共享相同的模型权重和 KV 缓存，仅需迁移所有权发生变化的切片即可完成切换。在 8x H200 GPU 上服务 Qwen3-235B-A22B 模型时，能在任意负载点匹敌最优静态并行策略，RL rollout 场景提升 1.16-1.25x 性能，切换耗时 215-434ms，内存开销仅 2.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型服务面临 TP 和 EP 两种并行策略各自的性能拐点：低并发时 TP 更快，高并发时 EP 更优。生产负载（在线服务的突发流量、RL rollout 的高并发衰减为长尾）会频繁跨越该边界，固定任何一种布局都会在负载切换时损失性能。因此需要一种能在运行时动态切换并行策略的系统。

Method: Moebius 的核心洞察是 EP 和 TP 是同一模型的不同布局（而非两个模型），它们对字节完全相同的专家权重和 KV 缓存计算相同函数，切换仅改变每个 rank 拥有的切片归属。系统通过融合 GPU-to-GPU 传输内核，在固定地址上重新分片单份专家权重和 KV 缓存，并在 decode 步骤之间完成切片迁移，无需排空正在执行的请求。两种并行模式的运行时保持不变，切换开销仅来自移动所有权发生改变的切片。

Result: 在 8x H200 GPU 上服务 Qwen3-235B-A22B 模型，Moebius 在所有负载点均匹配最优静态并行策略性能；RL rollout 场景下跨步骤提升 1.16-1.25x；每次切换耗时 215-434ms；同时保持两种布局运行时仅需 2.4% 额外内存开销。

Conclusion: Moebius 证明了 MoE 模型服务中 TP 和 EP 之间的动态切换是可行且高效的，能够在生产负载动态变化时自适应选择最优并行策略，同时以极低的内存开销和毫秒级切换延迟消除固定布局的性能损失。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) to hundreds of billions of parameters. Serving a single MoE model requires multiple GPUs operating in parallel, typically through tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP). The optimal choice depends on the number of in-flight requests: TP is faster at low concurrency, whereas EP wins at high concurrency. Production workloads cross this boundary continually: online serving sees bursty arrivals that subside into quiet periods, and reinforcement-learning rollouts begin as a high-concurrency burst that decays into a long tail of stragglers. Pinning either layout therefore forfeits performance when the workload crosses to the other side.
  We present Moebius, a serving system that switches between EP and TP at runtime without restarting the engine or dropping in-flight requests. Our key insight is that EP and TP are two layouts of one model, not two models: they compute the same function over byte-identical expert weights and KV cache, so a switch changes only which rank owns each slice. Moving those owner-changed slices is the sole irreducible cost, and modern high-bandwidth GPU interconnects make it fast enough to do between decode steps without draining in-flight requests. Moebius preserves each parallelism's runtime resident, and reshards the single copy of expert weights and KV cache at fixed addresses with fused GPU-to-GPU transfer kernels. On 8x H200 GPUs serving Qwen3-235B-A22B, Moebius matches the better static parallelism at every operating point, and beats it on RL rollouts by 1.16-1.25x across steps. Each switch completes in 215-434 ms, and Moebius holds both layouts resident with only 2.4% memory overhead.

</details>


### [101] [Simulating Unified Tensor Resharding in heterogeneous AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26633)
*Sumit Kumar,Sayantan Dasgupta,Kushal Mitra,Meet Dadhania,Rohan Sudhir Basugade,Praveen Tammana,Satananda Burla,Abed Mohammad Kamaluddin,Rinku Shah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Xsim是一个面向异构分布式LLM训练的模拟器，支持异构感知的负载均衡、通信优化、流水线并行抽象和灵活部署配置，在真实异构部署场景下预测训练时间误差低于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI训练模拟器假设同质化计算和网络基础设施，但现实中训练基础设施日益异构化，原因包括：多模态和MoE等模型架构利用异构性提升设备利用率、公有云同质硬件可用性有限、大型企业常部署地理分布式异构基础设施。

Method: Xsim支持五项核心设计：(i) 跨异构设备组的非均匀工作负载分区实现负载均衡；(ii) 通过自定义环构建和分块划分实现异构感知的集合通信；(iii) 可复用的异构感知抽象用于流水线并行算法和非均匀张量重分片技术；(iv) 灵活输入抽象支持自定义设备组和设备-并行映射的部署方案；(v) 可插拔集成NS-3和htsim，支持模拟保真度与性能/可扩展性的权衡。

Result: Xsim在真实异构部署中预测训练时间误差低于5%（大多数异构数据并行/张量并行配置），流水线并行通信建模误差约2%，并提供了流水线气泡时间和落后者等待时间等可操作指标。

Conclusion: Xsim有效解决了异构分布式LLM训练的模拟需求，通过异构感知设计实现了高精度预测，支持用户根据需求在模拟保真度和性能之间灵活权衡。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI training simulators assume homogeneous compute and network infrastructure. However, real-world training infrastructure is becoming increasingly heterogeneous since: (a) Model architectures such as multimodal and MoE exploit heterogeneity to improve device utilization, (b) Public cloud platforms often provide limited availability of homogeneous hardware due to fast hardware evolution, and (c) Large enterprises frequently deploy geographically distributed infrastructure that is both diverse and heterogeneous. In this paper, we present Xsim, a heterogeneity-aware simulator for distributed LLM training. Xsim supports: (i) Load balancing through non-uniform workload partitioning across heterogeneous device groups, (ii) Heterogeneity-aware collective communication via customized ring construction and chunk partitioning, (iii) Reusable heterogeneity-aware abstractions for emerging pipeline-parallel algorithms and non-uniform tensor resharding technique, (iv) Flexible input abstractions for specifying deployment plans with custom device groups and custom device-to-parallelism mappings, and (v) Pluggable integration with NS-3 and htsim, allowing users to trade off simulation fidelity for performance and scalability. Our evaluation demonstrates that Xsim accurately predicts training time for real-world heterogeneous deployments, with an error of less than 5% across most heterogeneous data-parallel/tensor-parallel configurations and around 2% error with pipeline-parallel communication modeling. We expose actionable metrics such as pipeline bubble time and straggler waiting time.

</details>


### [102] [RolloutPipe: Overlapping Pipelined Rollout and Training in Disaggregated On-Policy LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26997)
*Rongjian Chen,Jianmin Hu,Kejiang Ye,Minxian Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出RolloutPipe框架，通过完整组流水线（CGP）和前沿组调度（FGD）技术，在解耦式RLVR系统中实现生成与训练的重叠，在保持策略在线正确性的前提下，将卷展到训练结束时间缩短30.7%-42.3%，训练器等待比例降低37%-76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同步GRPO系统需完成全部卷展生成后才开始训练，导致训练GPU池在生成阶段闲置；异步流水线虽能重叠两阶段，但需承担陈旧数据训练的代价。亟需一种既能重叠生成与训练、又能保持在线策略正确性的方法。

Method: 提出RolloutPipe框架，包含两项核心技术：1）完整组流水线（CGP）：一旦卷展组完成物化，立即按FIFO顺序将该可训练完整组调度至训练器；2）前沿组调度（FGD）：在卷展节点上优先接受形成下一训练批次所需的前沿组请求，使训练器就绪组更早、更稳定地到达。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B模型上，跨4个推理与科学基准和12种卷展设置，RolloutPipe相比SOTA系统Slime，卷展到训练结束时间缩短30.7%-42.3%，训练器等待比例降低37%-76%。

Conclusion: RolloutPipe通过在解耦式RLVR系统中引入完整组流水线和前沿组调度，有效解决了生成与训练阶段资源闲置问题，同时保持策略的在线正确性，显著提升了分布式强化学习后训练的效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training for reasoning increasingly relies on reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where models learn from ground-truth feedback on mathematical, logical, and scientific tasks. To enable flexible resource allocation and support heterogeneous training setups, modern RLVR systems adopt disaggregated architectures that decouple rollout generation and policy training across independent GPU pools. However, existing synchronous on-policy GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) RLVR systems finish an entire rollout before starting training, leaving the trainer GPU pool idle while rollout is still ongoing. Asynchronous RL pipelines overlap the two stages, but at the cost of training on stale data. To address these challenges, we propose RolloutPipe, a post-training framework for disaggregated RLVR systems, which turns the fixed-weight rollout into a complete-group pipeline where trainable groups move to the trainer while later groups are still being generated. RolloutPipe achieves this through two techniques including complete-group pipelining (CGP) and frontier-group dispatch (FGD). CGP dispatches each trainable complete group to the trainer FIFO as soon as group materialization finishes, and FGD is an admission policy on the Rollout node that first admits requests for the frontier groups needed to form the next training batch, so that trainer-ready groups arrive earlier and more steadily. The design starts training before the rollout completes while maintaining on-policy correctness. Evaluated on Qwen3-1.7B across four reasoning and science benchmarks and twelve rollout settings, RolloutPipe shortens the rollout-to-train-end time by 30.7%-42.3%, and lowers the trainer waiting ratio by 37%-76% compared to Slime, a state-of-the-art rollout and training system.

</details>


### [103] [DMuon: Efficient Distributed Muon Training with Near-Adam Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27153)
*Vincent Chen,Starrick Liu,Regis Cheng,Dance Yang,Shalfun Li,Ryan Yu,Lucy Liang,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DMuon是一个开源分布式矩阵正交化优化器实现，通过将Muon优化器的额外计算开销从2倍以上降低到接近AdamW水平，在具体基础模型和大语言模型训练中实现了1.48x-3.01x的端到端加速和6.85x-163.00x的优化器步加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式训练基础设施基于逐元素优化器设计，与Muon等矩阵级优化器不匹配。Muon的更新涉及整个权重矩阵耦合且需要昂贵的Newton-Schulz迭代，导致其计算开销超过前向和反向传播的2倍，亟需高效分布式实现来弥合这一差距。

Method: DMuon作为一个可即插即用的开源分布式模块，无需修改框架层面代码即可集成到现有训练流水线中。通过优化分布式矩阵正交化计算和Newton-Schulz迭代的分布式执行，显著降低了优化器步骤的延迟。

Result: 在具体基础模型和大语言模型训练中：端到端步时间加速1.48x-3.01x，优化器步时间加速6.85x-163.00x，将每步延迟降低到接近AdamW水平。

Conclusion: DMuon有效解决了矩阵级优化器在分布式训练中的效率瓶颈，使得Muon这类具有强收敛特性的优化器能够在现代大规模异构模型训练中高效扩展，弥合了矩阵正交化优化器与现有分布式基础设施之间的差距。

Abstract: Matrix-orthogonalization-based optimizers, exemplified by Muon, have demonstrated strong convergence behavior across a wide range of modern deep learning workloads. The matrix-aware updates offer a compelling alternative to conventional element-wise optimization, particularly as model architectures continue to grow in scale and heterogeneity. Yet contemporary distributed training infrastructure built around the assumption of element-wise optimizers is poorly matched to matrix-level optimizers such as Muon, whose updates couple entire weight matrices and require costly Newton-Schulz iterations. Vanilla Muon implementations incur more than 2x the cost of forward and backward passes. To close this gap, we present DMuon, an open-source distributed Muon implementation that integrates into existing training pipelines as a drop-in module, with no framework-level modifications. Across both embodied foundation model and large language model (LLM) training workloads, DMuon achieves a 1.48x-3.01x speedup in end-to-end step time and a 6.85x-163.00x speedup in optimizer-step time, bringing per-step latency to near-AdamW levels and enabling efficient scaling in our model training.

</details>


### [104] [CHAMB-GA: A Containerized HPC Scalable Microservice-Based Framework for Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27217)
*Felix Bonhoff,Thiemo Pesch,Andrea Benigni,Alexander Mitsos,Manuel Dahmen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAMB-GA，一个基于容器化、微服务架构、可扩展至HPC的遗传算法框架，支持从个人电脑到分布式集群的无缝迁移，通过消息代理协调异步通信，在3500+CPU核上验证了可扩展性，并成功应用于德国高压直流输电线路调度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于元启发式算法且包含嵌入式长时间仿真的全局优化计算成本高昂，现有工具链刚性且受硬件约束，难以实现从个人电脑到分布式集群的无缝迁移，限制了可扩展性和复杂仿真的集成。

Method: 提出CHAMB-GA框架：采用容器化部署和微服务架构，通过容器编排（如Kubernetes）和批调度（如SLURM）实现跨云和HPC集群的一致扩展；用户分别提供GA算子和仿真后端；中央消息代理协调异步管理者-工作者通信，并行化进化操作和适应度评估；适应度评估和遗传操作解耦至不同硬件资源。

Result: 基准测试表明框架在扩展至3500+CPU核时开销极小；在德国高压直流输电线路优化中展示了从Kubernetes到SLURM的无缝迁移、水平和垂直联合扩展、以及多阶段工作流集成能力。

Conclusion: CHAMB-GA具有良好的可扩展性、可移植性和可复现性，能够有效集成外部工具和复杂仿真，为大规模分布式进化计算提供了灵活的框架支撑。

Abstract: Metaheuristic-based global optimization with embedded, long-running simulations is a computationally expensive process. To support various stages of development and execution, a seamless transition from personal computers to distributed clusters is desired, enabling execution across all computational scales. However, existing tool chains are often characterized by rigidity and hardware-bound constraints, which impede scalability and the integration of complex simulations. Bridging this gap, we present a containerized HPC scalable microservice-based framework for genetic algorithms with embedded simulations (CHAMB-GA). The deployment of the framework scales consistently across cloud infrastructure via container orchestration and HPC clusters via batch-scheduled parallel execution. Users provide the GA operators and simulation backend separately. The framework is designed to run these components in a distributed and decoupled manner, mapped to separate hardware. This approach ensures that the fitness evaluation and genetic operations are not managed within the same process and are utilizing distinct parts of the compute infrastructure. A central message broker coordinates asynchronous manager-worker communication between microservices, thereby parallelizing evolutionary operations and fitness evaluations. We demonstrate CHAMB-GA's scalability, portability, and reproducibility, while facilitating the integration of external tools and complex simulations on benchmark and powerflow problems. The capabilities of CHAMB-GA are validated in a two-part approach: (i) a benchmark study demonstrating minimal overhead while scaling to over 3,500 CPU cores, and (ii) a dispatch optimization of High Voltage Direct Current (HVDC) lines in the German transmission grid, showing seamless migration from Kubernetes to SLURM, combined horizontal and vertical scaling, and integration of multi-stage workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代数据生成管线，通过提取随行为线性缩放的特征样本（而非简单二元对比样本），更好地解耦和操控语言模型中的谄媚行为（sycophancy），在检测、评分和引导方面匹配或超越基线方法，且计算成本更低、可解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活引导方法依赖二元对比样本来解释和控制模型行为，但二元样本难以有效解耦复杂行为背后的线性特征，导致特征检测不可靠、引导效果受限。

Method: 提出迭代数据生成管线，通过逐步筛选出行为程度线性变化的级联样本（cascading samples），发现与谄媚行为线性相关的特征子空间，并基于这些特征进行检测、确定性评分和鲁棒引导。

Result: 级联样本发现的谄媚特征形成线性可分子空间，能更清晰地对期望行为对应的模型激活进行选择；在检测、评分和引导任务上匹配或超越LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，且计算需求更低、可解释性更强。

Conclusion: 超越二元对比样本、采用线性级联样本能够更有效地解耦和操控模型行为，为可解释性和安全对齐提供了更具可扩展性的方法。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [106] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批评了以准确率为中心的基准评估范式，提出应从构造效度、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架相对重要性、人机协作提升等六个维度评估智能体性能。以CORE-Bench Hard（科学代码计算可复现性基准）为案例，展示了即使准确率饱和，多维评估仍能提供有意义 insights，并提出了改进版基准CORE-Bench v1.1、分布外测试集CORE-Bench OOD，以及人机协作实验发现约两倍的统计显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准评估过度聚焦准确率，当准确率饱和后便退休换新，忽视了其他关键性能维度。作者希望提出更严格的替代评估范式，在准确率饱和后仍能获得有意义的智能体性能洞察。

Method: 以CORE-Bench Hard为案例，从六个维度（构造效度、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型vs脚手架重要性、人机协作提升）进行分析。识别构造效度威胁后提出CORE-Bench v1.1和OOD测试集；测量饱和后的效率与可靠性；进行小规模随机实验测量人机协作提升。

Result: 1) 发现了在弱智能体下难以预见的构造效度威胁；2) 准确率饱和后，CORE-Bench v1.1仍可用于测量效率、可靠性、模型性能和脚手架性能；3) 人机协作实现约两倍的统计显著加速（可能被低估，因为五分之一纯人类复现因时间限制未完成）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种比传统准确率中心评估范式更严格的替代方案，强调多维评估在基准饱和后的持续价值。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [107] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调模型中的拒绝机制（refusal）与顺从人格（compliant persona）之间存在交互：顺从人格会门控（gate）拒绝行为的表达。通过方向干预实验，证明了拒绝行为在后期层的表达阶段受到人格方向的调节，仅将拒绝视为独立方向会忽略其对人格的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
